Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: B

Variant: C

Order Number: 1

Order Number: 1

Variant: C

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Anya - A

Dialogue: "I used to worry that people could disappear from your life without you noticing. I don't worry about that with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-anya-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Anya - B

Dialogue: "Some people make a place feel different just by being there. Blackmere feels different with you in it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-anya-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Anya - C

Dialogue: "I've known you long enough that even ordinary days with you feel worth remembering."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-anya-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Anya - A

Dialogue: "You aren't just someone I'll remember someday. You're someone I'm happy to know right now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-anya-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: A

---

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Anya Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "Oh! Hi. Um...did you need something?"

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "I was just looking at something. It's nothing important, though."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Felix - C

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "Look what I found! Oh...sorry. You probably don't want to see every weird rock I pick up."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "Do you collect anything? You don't have to answer. I was just wondering."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "You came back! I wasn't sure if we'd talk again."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "I found three things today! A striped feather, a really smooth stone, and...well, the third one's kind of hard to explain."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "Hey, do you want to hear something interesting? I've been waiting for someone to tell."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "I almost went farther down the river today. Almost. Maybe tomorrow I'll actually do it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "There you are! I found something yesterday and thought, 'I bet they'd want to see this.'"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "I've decided you can see the good collection. Milo's usually the only one who gets to see that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "Want to know something? I don't get as nervous talking to you anymore."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "Okay, imagine there's a hidden cave somewhere around Blackmere. Where would you look first? I've been thinking about this all morning."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "I added something new to my collection! I knew you'd want to hear about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "Milo says I think too much before doing things. He's probably right. You'd still come exploring with me, wouldn't you?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "I've been thinking...maybe being nervous about something doesn't mean you shouldn't try it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "If I ever actually become an explorer, you can't laugh when I get lost five minutes into my first expedition."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "You always listen when I talk about the things I find. It makes me want to keep looking for more."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "Guess what? I tried something today before I could talk myself out of it. It actually went pretty well!"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "I have a new rule: if I find something amazing, you're one of the first people who gets to see it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "Talking to you is easy. I don't have to worry whether you'll think the things I like are silly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "Perfect timing! I need a second opinion. Feather, strange leaf, or mysterious piece of bark?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "I saw you coming and almost ran over to show you what I found. I managed to walk instead."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "I've been planning an expedition. Nothing dangerous! Probably. Anyway, I already put your name on the list."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "I've started writing down where I find everything. Someday we could look through it and remember all the places we've explored."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "You know what makes finding something new even better? Having somebody who gets excited about it with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "If we ever find something nobody in Blackmere has seen before, we have to name it together. That's only fair."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "I've been looking at maps of places outside Blackmere again. They don't seem quite as scary as they used to."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "One day I'm going to have shelves full of things I've discovered. You'd better remember how the collection started."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "I think adventures are less frightening when you know there's someone you can tell about them afterward."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "Hey! I've got an idea for our next adventure. Yes, I said 'our.' You're involved now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "I used to hide the things I cared about because I thought people might laugh. I never feel like I have to hide them from you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "There are still places I'm scared to explore. But if you were there too...I think I'd go anyway."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Felix - A

Dialogue: "Someday, when I'm a real explorer, I'll tell everyone my first adventures happened right here in Blackmere. And you were part of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-felix-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Felix - B

Dialogue: "I used to think being brave meant going somewhere nobody else had gone. Now I think it can mean having someone you trust enough to go with."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-felix-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Felix - C

Dialogue: "I've found a lot of treasures around Blackmere. Getting a friend like you might be my best discovery yet."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-felix-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Felix Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "Hey! Quick question: how good are you at climbing? Actually, never mind. I'll find out eventually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "I've been trying to figure you out. New inventor moves into an old manor? There's definitely a story there."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "Just so we're clear, if you discover a secret passage anywhere in Blackmere, I expect to hear about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "You haven't scared me off yet, and I haven't scared you off. I'd call that a promising start."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "I've decided you're interesting. Congratulations! That's harder to accomplish around here than you'd think."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "Saw you wandering around earlier. You should try leaving the roads sometime. That's where Blackmere gets good."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "All right, you've officially earned provisional adventure-partner status. Don't get excited. There's a very rigorous evaluation process."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "I've got three places I want to investigate and enough daylight for maybe two. See the problem? Exactly. We need to move."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "You're more adventurous than I expected. Or you're terrible at saying no to me. Either one works."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "Good, you're here! I need somebody sensible enough to tell me when an idea is terrible, but fun enough to come along anyway."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "I've upgraded you from 'person I know' to 'person I actually look for.' That's a prestigious title."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "You know what I like about you? I don't have to explain why finding an unexplored trail is exciting. You just get it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "New rule: if either of us discovers something incredible, the other gets told immediately. No exceptions."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "I marked a trail on my map that I haven't explored yet. Every time I look at it, it's practically daring me to go."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "If I suddenly yell 'follow me,' are you going to ask questions first? Because that could seriously slow us down."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "Okay, apparently charging ahead isn't always the best strategy. Don't look so surprised. I'm capable of learning things."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "You ever notice how much you miss when you're trying to get somewhere fast? I've been testing this whole 'slowing down' thing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "I've started checking my maps twice before heading out. Twice! That's practically patience."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "Wait. Before we go anywhere, look around. Anything seem unusual to you? I've been practicing noticing things before running toward them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "I almost took off exploring without you earlier. Then I thought, 'Nope, this one needs two people.' So here we are."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "You're officially someone whose advice I listen to. Sometimes. Don't abuse this enormous privilege."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "Finally! I've been waiting for you. I could've gone without you, obviously, but where's the fun in that?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "I put your name on one of my maps. Not marking your house...I know where that is. I marked a place we need to explore together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "You know we've spent too much time together when I can already hear what you're going to say about my latest plan."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "I've been reorganizing my explorer's journal. Turns out half my best discoveries have you somewhere in the story."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "Here's something weird: lately I care less about being the first person to find something. I still want to find it, obviously. I'm not completely different."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "We're a pretty good team, aren't we? I charge ahead, you keep me from missing everything important, and somehow we both survive."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "I found this tiny trail yesterday that I'd walked past at least twenty times. Old me would've missed it twenty-one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "If I'm ever about to do something unbelievably reckless, you're allowed to stop me. You'll have to catch me first."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "I've explored places alone my whole life. Turns out having somebody beside you doesn't make it less adventurous. It makes the story better."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "I've got maps, supplies, two backup plans, and no intention of doing anything until you get here. Who am I becoming?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "There aren't many people I'd trust to follow me somewhere I've never been. You're at the top of a very short list."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "Someday I'm going to explore way beyond Blackmere. Doesn't matter how far I go, though. I'll always come back with stories for you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Kira - A

Dialogue: "I've decided something. Finding the greatest undiscovered place in Morvanya would be incredible, but finding it with my best friend? Better."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-kira-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Kira - B

Dialogue: "You know how I'm always worried I'll miss something if I stop moving? I don't feel that way when I'm with you. Whatever happens next, we'll find it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-kira-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Kira - C

Dialogue: "Come on, partner. Blackmere still has secrets left, and we've got plenty of time to find them. Turns out that's half the fun."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-kira-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Kira Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "Whoa! Stop right there! You're standing in lava. Lucky for you, I think that rock is safe."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "Hey! You're the new person, right? I'm Milo. I was going somewhere important, but I forgot where."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "Did you know there could be buried treasure anywhere? Under a tree, beside the river, underneath your feet...probably not underneath your feet."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "Oh, hey! Today I'm hunting the Great Graythorne River Monster. I haven't decided what it looks like yet."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "Quick! Pick a direction! Doesn't matter which one. That's where the adventure is now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "I remember you! I remember almost everybody, but I remembered you really fast."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "Excellent! My backup has arrived. I don't know what we're doing yet, but now there are two of us doing it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "I've decided you're trustworthy. If anyone asks, we definitely didn't build a secret fort behind the hay bales."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "You need an adventurer name. Everybody does. I'll think of one when something amazing happens."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "Captain! You're late! The expedition started five minutes ago, and we've already lost all our supplies. Mostly because I forgot to bring any."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "Today's mission is extremely dangerous. We have to cross Mercer Farms without Mom giving me another chore."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "Good news! You're officially on my adventure team. Bad news! There are no meetings, rules, uniforms, or snacks."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "Yesterday, I defeated six bandits with nothing but a stick. Okay, they were fence posts. And they didn't fight back. Still counts."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "I found the perfect climbing tree! Kira says I've climbed it before, but that was a different adventure."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "Okay, imagine we're explorers, the farm is an ancient kingdom, and that chicken over there is guarding the castle. See it? Now you can't unsee it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "I found my first adventure journal! Some of the stories aren't exactly how I remember them, but I think that makes them better."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "Want to hear something funny? Half my greatest adventures happened right here on the farm. I didn't even have to go anywhere!"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "I used to draw Kira in almost every adventure in my journal. I think you're starting to show up a lot too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "Attention! Today's adventure has one rule: nobody runs ahead. I made that rule for me, mostly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "I spent forever watching this frog earlier. It didn't even do anything! Somehow, it was still pretty great."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "Usually when I finish one adventure, I start another right away. Lately I've been trying to remember the first one before I do."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "There you are! Now the story can start. I tried beginning without you, but it wasn't as good."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "I've got a stick sword for me, another one for you, and absolutely no idea what we're fighting. We'll figure that part out."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "I told Felix about something we did the other day. He said I exaggerated it. I only added one dragon!"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "I've been saving little things from our adventures for my journal. Don't worry, I haven't saved any bugs. Well...not many."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "Chapter Twenty-Seven: Milo and his fearless companion face their greatest challenge yet! I haven't decided what the challenge is, but that's definitely the title."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "You know what my journal needed? Another main character. Good thing you showed up."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "I was going to go looking for something exciting today. Then I started skipping stones with everybody and forgot. Best failed adventure ever."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "Maybe adventures don't need monsters and treasure every time. Yesterday we just sat around talking, and I still wrote about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "I've been trying to remember the little parts of our adventures too. Turns out those are usually the funniest parts."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "Okay, today's plan is...nothing! We can just see what happens. Apparently that's allowed."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "I used to hate waiting because I thought I'd miss something fun. But some of my favorite days happened because I stuck around."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "If somebody asked me about my best adventures, I'd probably tell them about the people first now. Weird, huh?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Milo - A

Dialogue: "I figured it out! The best adventures aren't the ones where the most stuff happens. They're the ones you wish weren't over yet."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-milo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Milo - B

Dialogue: "Someday my adventure journal is going to be completely full. Good thing I know exactly who'll be in a lot of the stories."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-milo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Milo - C

Dialogue: "Hey, best friend! No mission today. No treasure map either. I just wanted to hang out. That's an adventure too, right?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-milo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Milo Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "Good morning. Or afternoon, perhaps. Sorry, I've been reading for several hours and seem to have lost track."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "Hello. I believe we've passed one another a few times, though we've never really spoken at length."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "Ah, hello. If you're looking for something, I may be able to help. Depending on what it is, of course."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "You're becoming a familiar face. That's meant positively, in case my phrasing suggested otherwise."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "I remembered something you mentioned the other day. I nearly wrote it down before realizing that might be somewhat peculiar."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "Hello again. I'm beginning to suspect our conversations aren't entirely coincidental anymore."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I came across something yesterday that reminded me of our last conversation. Rather unexpectedly, actually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "I've enjoyed our conversations. I considered saying that sooner, but apparently I needed several days to determine whether it sounded strange."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "You ask interesting questions. Ones I occasionally continue thinking about long after you've gone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "There you are. I was hoping you might stop by today. Though I hadn't planned what I'd say if you actually did."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "I've noticed I'm considerably less distracted when we talk. Which is surprising, because conversation usually has the opposite effect."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "I suppose we've become friends, haven't we? That seems obvious now that I've said it aloud."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I nearly brought you a book I thought you'd enjoy. Then I reconsidered the choice three times. It's still sitting on my desk."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "Do you ever begin telling someone something simply because you know they'll understand? I've caught myself doing that with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "It's rather pleasant having someone around who doesn't mind how long it sometimes takes me to reach a point."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I've spent so much time worrying about saying the wrong thing that I occasionally forget saying nothing can be worse."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "You have an unfortunate habit of making me reconsider things I thought I'd already settled. I mean that as a compliment."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "I trust your perspective. More than I expected to, if I'm being entirely truthful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I made a decision this morning without researching every possible consequence first. Remarkably, civilization appears to have survived."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "You've become someone I seek out when my thoughts become tangled. Somehow they seem much simpler after we talk."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "I had something important to tell you. For once, I'm going to resist rehearsing it beforehand."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I was hoping I'd see you. There. No qualification necessary."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "There are very few people around whom silence feels as comfortable as conversation. You're one of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "I've realized I tell you things before I've completely sorted out what I think about them. That's rather unusual for me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I showed you work I normally wouldn't let anyone read. Strangely, I was more interested in what you'd think than whether you'd find a mistake."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "You've read enough of my notes by now to know how often I change my mind. Thank you for never making me feel foolish for it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "I've started writing with the assumption that someone else might actually read it someday. I think you had something to do with that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I caught myself wondering what you'd say about something before deciding what I thought. Then I realized I already knew my answer."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "You know me remarkably well by now. I should probably find that intimidating. Instead, I find it comforting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "Somehow you've become part of my routine without either of us arranging it. I rather like that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "There are things I can tell you without wondering how they'll sound first. I didn't realize how rare that was until you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "I spent years believing certainty had to come before trust. Apparently, sometimes trust is what allows certainty to follow."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "Whatever happens next, I'm glad you're someone I can face it with. I don't need to reconsider that statement."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I've spent enough time analyzing what you mean to me. The answer is actually quite simple: you're one of the most important people in my life."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "For someone who values written history, I've become surprisingly interested in whatever chapter comes next."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "I trust you. Completely. It's taken me a long time to become comfortable saying things that simply."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I still catch myself smiling when I remember we're going home to the same place. I suspect that feeling may take some time to become ordinary."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "Marriage has apparently made my books migrate to every available surface in the Manor. Please tell me if I've occupied an unreasonable number."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "I thought sharing a home might take considerable adjustment. Instead, having you there feels strangely natural."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I left my notebook at home this morning. Normally that would bother me terribly. Then I remembered exactly where it was, and who it was waiting beside."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "I've begun thinking of my days differently. There's work, the library, whatever Blackmere requires...and then there's coming home to you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "You know every version of me by now...the thoughtful one, the worried one, the one who revises a sentence six times. Somehow you chose all of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Adrian - A

Dialogue: "I used to believe the most important thing I could leave behind was a complete history of Blackmere. Now I'd rather make a life worth writing about with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-adrian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Adrian - B

Dialogue: "For once, I don't need a notebook to preserve something important. I think I'll remember this life with you perfectly well on my own."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-adrian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Adrian - C

Dialogue: "I've spent most of my life preparing for every possible future. I don't do that nearly as much anymore. Whatever comes next, we'll decide together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-adrian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Adrian Lockwood

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "You're settling into Blackmere quickly. Faster than I expected, actually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "I've noticed you rarely stay idle for long. Good. Neither does this town."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor suits you. Don't ask me to elaborate yet. I'm still assessing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "First impression revised. You're considerably more capable than I initially gave you credit for."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "You have an interesting way of approaching problems. Not how I'd do it, but the results are difficult to argue with."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "I've been watching what you do around town. Professionally curious, before you make that expression."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "You're reliable. That's a useful quality, and a surprisingly uncommon one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "I've decided I like working through ideas with you. You challenge the right parts."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "There was a flaw in one of my assumptions about you. I've corrected it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I've started leaving room in my day for our conversations. Don't make me regret the scheduling decision."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "You know you've become a friend when I stop pretending you're interrupting me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "Stay a moment. I could use a conversation that has absolutely nothing to do with load-bearing walls."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I changed a design because of something you said. You may appreciate how rarely that sentence leaves my mouth."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "I've discovered a problem with asking your opinion: occasionally, yours is better."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "You're good at seeing what I'm trying to accomplish before I finish explaining it. That's useful. Slightly unnerving, but useful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I scrapped three revisions last night and went back to the original design. Please appreciate how monumental that is."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "You once told me something was already good enough. I hated that advice. I'm beginning to suspect you were right."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "Apparently there is a point where improving something starts making it worse. I'd prefer if you didn't look so pleased that I've admitted it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I finished something yesterday and didn't immediately look for flaws. I lasted nearly ten minutes."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "You've become my favorite second opinion. Mara may object to losing the position."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "Sometimes you see potential where I see unfinished work. I'm trying to decide which of us has the healthier perspective."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "Good. I wanted you here. There's something I've been thinking about, and you're the person I want to test it against."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "I can usually predict what you'll criticize now. More troublingly, I can predict when you'll be correct."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "There are plans I won't show most people until they're polished. You get to see the ugly sketches."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I've started designing around how people actually live instead of how I think they should. You helped with that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "My locked portfolio has fewer designs in it now. Some of them deserved daylight more than another revision."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "You were right about unfinished work. Sometimes the only thing missing is the courage to call it finished."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I caught myself sketching something because I thought you'd like it. No practical justification whatsoever. Disturbing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "You've become part of how I picture Blackmere's future. I hadn't planned for that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "Some days I work too long because stopping feels like wasting time. Then you appear, and suddenly stopping doesn't feel wasteful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I've designed buildings meant to last generations. Somehow planning next week with you feels more significant."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "I know your flaws by now. You know mine. Neither of us seems particularly interested in redesigning the other."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "There are very few things in my life I don't feel compelled to improve. What we have is one of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "For once, I have no revisions. I like who I am when you're part of my life."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "I spent years thinking anything worthwhile needed a perfect plan. Then you became important to me without one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "Whatever we're building here, I don't need to see the finished design. I already know it's worth the work."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "Living together has revealed one serious design flaw: apparently I require far more table space than any reasonable person."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "I've drawn buildings my entire life. Strange that a place didn't truly feel like home until I started sharing one with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "Marriage suits us. Efficient partnership, complementary strengths, excellent long-term prospects. Also, I love you. That seems relevant."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I had an entire evening of work planned yesterday. Then we spent it together instead. I wouldn't change the schedule."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "There are little things about our life I'd never have thought to design. Those may be my favorite parts."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "I've stopped imagining how our life could be better. Not because it couldn't change, but because I actually remember to enjoy it now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Clara - A

Dialogue: "I used to want my greatest work to be something Blackmere remembered long after I was gone. Now I think a good life with you would be legacy enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-clara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Clara - B

Dialogue: "No blueprint. No final revision. No perfect version waiting somewhere ahead. Just us, building this as we go. I like that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-clara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Clara - C

Dialogue: "I've finally found something I don't want to finish. This life we're building can stay a work in progress."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-clara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Clara Weiss

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Ah, the new resident of Pendrelle Manor. Your arrival has prompted a considerable amount of discussion throughout Blackmere. Newcomers have a remarkable way of disrupting a town's established narrative, though I assure you I mean that as an observation rather than a criticism."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "We haven't been properly acquainted for very long, have we? An interesting circumstance. One can know a surprising amount about someone's place in a community while knowing remarkably little about the person themselves."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "You must forgive me if I occasionally seem preoccupied when we speak. Occupational habit. Curators are trained to observe details, and regrettably there is no convenient switch with which to deactivate the tendency."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "I've noticed you becoming increasingly integrated into Blackmere's daily affairs. It's fascinating how quickly a person can transition from being referred to as 'the newcomer' to simply being addressed by name."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "People often assume history is composed primarily of momentous events. In reality, most change begins rather inconspicuously...with a new neighbor, a repaired building, a conversation repeated often enough to become familiar."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I confess, when you first arrived I wondered whether Blackmere would prove merely temporary for you. I am beginning to suspect I underestimated your capacity for putting down roots."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "You've become a recurring figure in my observations of Blackmere's recovery. That sounds distressingly clinical. What I mean is that your presence has become increasingly difficult to separate from the changes occurring around town."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "There is an archival principle stating that repetition establishes significance. By that measure, considering how frequently we seem to encounter one another, I suppose our conversations have officially become significant."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I had intended to spend today cataloguing several recovered artifacts. Instead, I've found myself wondering what you might make of them. Apparently your perspective has become another variable in my research."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "A curious development: I find myself considerably more inclined to explain things when you're present. Given my existing tendency toward excessive explanation, you may reasonably regard this as a threat."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "Friendship is rarely documented with any precision. There is no definitive date upon which acquaintance becomes familiarity and familiarity becomes affection. Nevertheless, I believe we have crossed at least one of those thresholds."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "You've acquired the unusual distinction of being someone I don't mind interrupting my work. Considering how protective I am of uninterrupted research, I hope you recognize the magnitude of that statement."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "I came across an artifact this morning whose purpose remains uncertain. My immediate thought was that you would enjoy examining it. Only afterward did I realize how naturally that thought occurred."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "There is considerable value in having one's interpretations challenged by another person. You have done so several times now, sometimes deliberately and sometimes merely by asking a question I neglected to consider."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I've begun recognizing which exhibits would interest you before you even enter the gallery. Perhaps familiarity is simply another form of careful study, albeit a considerably more pleasant one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Preservation has always seemed straightforward to me: identify what matters, protect it, ensure it survives. Creating something worthy of preservation oneself is a far more intimidating proposition."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "You have an irritating talent for making possibilities sound achievable. I assure you, 'irritating' is being employed affectionately in this instance."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I've spent years surrounded by evidence of extraordinary people accomplishing extraordinary things. You occasionally make me wonder whether they felt extraordinary while doing them. Perhaps they simply began."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "I drafted an exhibition proposal yesterday. Not notes for one. Not preliminary research. An actual proposal. I haven't submitted it yet, admittedly, but progress needn't become reckless."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "You once suggested that Blackmere's present deserves as much attention as its past. I initially considered that somewhat simplistic. I have since concluded that I was being somewhat insufferable."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "Curating teaches one to recognize significance after the fact. Knowing something matters while it is still happening is considerably more difficult. I'm attempting to become better at that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "There you are. I encountered something fascinating earlier and have been mentally constructing an explanation for you ever since. I apologize in advance for its probable length."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "I've noticed that my favorite conversations with you rarely concern the subject with which they began. We start with history, wander through several unrelated ideas, and somehow arrive somewhere worth reaching."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I used to think inspiration was something one discovered in remarkable objects. Lately I suspect people may be considerably more effective at providing it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "I've begun documenting parts of Blackmere's recovery that I previously considered too ordinary for exhibition. You helped me realize that ordinary and insignificant are not remotely synonymous."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "Future historians may eventually study this period and assign tremendous significance to decisions that currently seem mundane. I find that thought strangely encouraging. Perhaps importance doesn't always announce itself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "There are ideas I would never have pursued before knowing you. Not because you supplied the ideas yourself, but because you somehow convinced me mine deserved pursuit."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "I've spent my career asking what objects reveal about the people who possessed them. Recently I've caught myself wondering what ordinary things might someday remind me of you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "You occupy a rather unusual position in my thoughts. Somewhere between trusted confidant, intellectual provocateur, and the person I most frequently hope will walk through the door."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "History records accomplishments with admirable efficiency and relationships rather poorly. A shame, really. The latter seem increasingly responsible for making the former possible."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "There are achievements I once imagined would make a life meaningful. I still value them, certainly, but I no longer believe accomplishment alone is sufficient. You complicated that theory rather thoroughly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "I find myself wanting you present for things before I know whether they'll prove important. That may be the clearest indication yet that importance was never really the point."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "If someone catalogued my life with the same diligence I apply to this museum, I suspect your name would begin appearing with increasingly embarrassing frequency."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "I've devoted much of my life to determining what deserves to be remembered. Regarding you, at least, the question requires remarkably little deliberation."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "You changed my understanding of legacy. I once thought it meant leaving something behind that endured for centuries. Now I think influencing even one life can be equally profound."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "For someone who spends an extraordinary amount of time discussing the past, you've made me unexpectedly enthusiastic about the future."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "This morning I found one of your things beside one of mine and experienced the peculiar realization that neither looked misplaced. Marriage seems to transform clutter into evidence of a shared existence."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "Historians possess an unfortunate tendency to document ceremonies while neglecting what follows them. Personally, I find the ordinary days after our wedding considerably more interesting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I had expected marriage to feel momentous. It does, certainly, but not in the manner I anticipated. The significance seems to reside in very small things: shared mornings, familiar footsteps, knowing I'll see you again at home."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "No museum would preserve the cup you left on the table this morning, the conversation we had over breakfast, or the way you made me laugh before I left. Yet those are precisely the things I found myself remembering today."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Dante - B

Dialogue: "I've begun keeping fewer notes about certain moments. An alarming development for a curator, perhaps, but I discovered that sometimes documenting an experience is less important than actually remaining present for it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "Our life together would make a dreadful museum exhibition. Too many mundane details, far too many private jokes, no coherent chronology whatsoever. Fortunately, I have no desire to curate it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Dante - A

Dialogue: "For years I feared reaching the end of my life having preserved everyone else's history while contributing nothing of my own. I no longer worry about that. We are creating something every day, even when no one else will ever know it happened."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Dante - B
