Dialogue: "There was a time when I wanted desperately to create something worthy of the Hall of Wonder. I still intend to contribute something meaningful, but I've learned that my most valuable accomplishment needn't belong behind glass. A life built with you is quite sufficient."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Dante - C

Dialogue: "Perhaps this is sacrilege coming from a curator, but I no longer believe everything precious must be preserved forever. Some things need only be experienced fully while they are ours. Whatever years we are given together, I intend to be present for them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "There you are! I've seen you around, but never long enough to get a proper impression. Give me a moment. Hmm...interesting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "You know, you carry yourself like someone who's perpetually on the way to do something. Very purposeful. Slightly exhausting to watch."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I've been wondering what your style is. Not your clothes...your style. Everyone has one. I'll figure yours out eventually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Ah! I knew I'd seen that expression before. That's your 'I have twelve things to accomplish and somehow intend to do all of them' face."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "You're fun to observe. That sounded dreadful. I mean you have little habits I'm beginning to recognize."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I haven't decided which color reminds me of you yet. Don't worry, I'm taking the responsibility very seriously."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I've got you figured out a little better now. Not completely, mind you. People who can be understood in three conversations are terribly boring."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "You have this look when you're genuinely interested in something. Your whole face changes. It's charming, actually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "Oh, good! I was hoping we'd run into each other. You're becoming one of my more enjoyable interruptions."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Hold still. Yes...there it is. You're much more relaxed around me now. I was wondering when that would happen."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "I know that face! You came over because you actually wanted to talk to me. Careful, darling, people might think we're becoming friends."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I've finally decided: you aren't one color. You're an entire palette. Much more interesting that way."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I could design something for you now without asking a single question. Not because I know your measurements...I mean, I probably do...but because I'm starting to know you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "You never seem bothered when I'm being a bit much. Which is fortunate, because 'a bit much' is approximately my natural state."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "Most people have a version of themselves they present first. Yours is slipping around the edges. I like what's underneath."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Confession: sometimes when someone dislikes one of my designs, I can laugh it off beautifully. Then I remember every word three days later. Ridiculous, isn't it?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "People assume confidence means never caring what anyone thinks. If that's the requirement, I'm afraid I've been fraudulently advertising."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "It's strange. I spend all day encouraging people to wear whatever makes them feel like themselves, and I'm still learning to follow my own advice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I wore something today that I nearly changed before leaving home. Then I thought, 'No. I like it.' Revolutionary behavior, apparently."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "You've seen me cheerful, dramatic, irritated, uncertain...goodness, I'm giving you the complete collection."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I don't feel like I have to be entertaining when you're around. I still am, obviously. But now it's recreational."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "There you are! Come closer. I need to see your face properly. Yes, much better. I missed that face."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "I can tell what sort of day you're having before you say anything now. Don't look alarmed. Tailor's intuition."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "You're one of the few people I don't mentally adjust when I look at you. No different color, no different cut, nothing. You simply look like yourself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I opened that trunk of designs again. The very personal ones I've never displayed. They didn't look quite as frightening this time."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "I've been asking myself whether I actually dislike some of my old designs, or whether I'm simply afraid someone else will. Those are very different problems."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "There are things I've made that feel almost embarrassingly like me. I think you're the first person I've wanted to show them to rather than hide them from."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Someone criticized my work yesterday. It hurt. Then, astonishingly, the world continued turning and I still liked what I'd made."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "I've spent so much time helping people feel comfortable being seen. I never realized how lovely it would feel to have someone do the same for me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "You know what's wonderfully inconvenient about you? I can't pretend everything's fine when it isn't. You know me too well now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I think there's a difference between wanting to be admired and wanting to be understood. I used to confuse them rather badly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "You don't always agree with me. You don't always like everything I make. And somehow I've never once wondered whether that means you like me less."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "The safest person isn't someone who praises everything you do. It's someone who can see you disappointed, insecure, completely unpolished...and stays."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I spent years trying to make clothing that helped people recognize themselves in the mirror. You have this remarkable way of doing that for me without making anything at all."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "You know the bright version of me everyone sees and all the quieter pieces underneath. I don't think I've ever felt so completely known."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I've finally figured out your style, by the way. It's unmistakably yours. I suppose that's why I've become so fond of it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Do you know what nobody warns you about marriage? Your spouse gets to see what you look like before you've made yourself presentable. Somehow you've survived the experience."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "I caught myself choosing what to wear this morning based entirely on what I wanted. No audience to impress, no reaction to anticipate. Just me. And then I remembered I get to come home to someone who likes that person."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "You have now witnessed loose threads, abandoned sketches, fabric everywhere, and my hair first thing in the morning. Congratulations, darling. You've unlocked the least curated version of Leo Finch."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I love getting dressed up with you. I love going places with you. But I think I've become equally fond of the evenings when neither of us has anyone to impress."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Sometimes I look at you across the room and think, 'That's my spouse.' Then I get ridiculously pleased with myself, as though marrying you was some brilliant design decision."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I used to think vulnerability meant showing someone the parts of yourself that weren't beautiful. I had it backward. Being trusted with those parts of you is one of the most beautiful things there is."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I've made hundreds of things meant to help someone say, 'This is who I am.' I think our life together does that for me better than anything I've ever sewn."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Leo - B

Dialogue: "There will always be people who think I'm too loud, too colorful, too sentimental, too much of something. Let them. I come home to someone who never needed me to become less."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Leo - C

Dialogue: "No alterations. That's my professional assessment of us. We'll change, obviously...we're supposed to...but I wouldn't take in a seam or trim away a single strange little piece of what we've become together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "If you're heading beyond town, keep an eye on the trails. Some of them are still rough from the flood."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "Settling into Pendrelle Manor all right? If something around town feels unsafe, report it. I'd rather hear about a problem early."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "You're covering a lot of ground lately. Watch your footing and know your way back before you wander too far."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "You've been around enough that I don't need to give you the usual safety lecture. Most of it, anyway."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "If you notice anything unusual while you're working, tell me. You're in parts of Blackmere I don't always reach on patrol."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "You pay attention to your surroundings. Good habit. Makes my job considerably easier."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I checked that path you mentioned. You were right about the damage. Keep telling me when you spot things like that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "I've started trusting your reports without verifying every detail myself. Don't make me regret admitting that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "If something doesn't look right out there, use your judgment. You've proven you've got a decent head on your shoulders."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I was about to make another patrol. Since you're here, what have you seen today? Your perspective might save me a trip."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "You've become one of the people I know I can count on. That's not something I say lightly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "If I'm occupied and someone needs help, I trust you to step in. You don't need my permission first."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I could use your opinion on something. Not an order, not official business. Just tell me what you think."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "I've noticed people listen when you speak. That's useful, but it also means they'll remember what you do. Handle that trust carefully."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "You're good in a difficult situation. You don't wait around for someone else to solve it. I respect that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "Long patrol today. Before you ask, yes, I've eaten. Eventually you'll have to find another way to check whether I'm taking care of myself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "I know I don't have to handle everything personally. Knowing something and actually behaving accordingly are apparently separate skills."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "You can stop giving me that look. I'll take a break when this is done. A real one. That's the compromise."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I delegated two things this morning. Both were completed without catastrophe. I'm beginning to see why people recommend this."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "Something came up earlier, and my first thought was to find you. You've become rather dependable backup."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "You don't need me hovering over your shoulder. If I ask you to handle something, it's because I trust you to handle it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "Good, you're here. Nothing's wrong. I simply wanted someone I trust nearby for a few minutes."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "You've gotten annoyingly good at noticing when something's bothering me. Apparently years of ranger training didn't prepare me for that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "If I tell you I'm fine, you usually know whether I mean it. I'm still deciding whether that's reassuring or inconvenient."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I used to think asking for help meant I'd failed to prepare properly. I'm starting to understand that sometimes preparation means knowing who to call."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "There are decisions I still have to make alone. It's different now, though. I know I don't have to carry what comes afterward alone too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "If I ever start acting like the entire town rests on my shoulders again, remind me that's thirty people trying very hard not to be carried."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I've spent years being the person everyone looks for when something goes wrong. It's strange having someone I can look for too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "I trust you with more than work now. Bad days, doubts, mistakes...things I'd rather not put in an official report."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "You know, it's considerably easier to be strong when someone else knows you're allowed not to be."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "If I make the wrong call someday, I know you'll tell me. More importantly, I know you'll still be there afterward."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "I've stopped measuring myself against the sheriff Blackmere lost. The town didn't ask me to become Ian. It asked me to serve. I can do that my way."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "You trusted me before I was entirely certain I'd earned it. I don't think I've ever told you how much that mattered."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I don't need you behind me. I don't need you ahead of me either. Right beside me works just fine."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "Protecting people will always matter to me. You've taught me something else matters too: trusting them enough to let them protect what matters to you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "Whatever comes next, you're the person I'd choose at my side. No hesitation. No second assessment."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I'm still getting used to ending a patrol and thinking about going home instead of simply being done for the day. There's someone waiting there now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "Before you say anything, I won't be late tonight. That's not a promise I make casually, so you may hold me to it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "Marriage has created an unexpected problem. Apparently 'the sheriff needs me' is less convincing when my spouse knows perfectly well someone else can cover the patrol."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I sleep more soundly now. Took me a while to understand why. I spend all day making sure Blackmere is safe, then I come home and feel safe myself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "There are responsibilities I won't compromise on. There are also evenings with you I won't surrender to work anymore. I'm learning both can matter."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "I spent so long believing being dependable meant never needing anyone. Now the person I depend on most shares my home. Life has a sense of humor."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Madison - A

Dialogue: "I still intend to be there when Blackmere needs me. The difference is that I finally understand my life can't consist entirely of being needed. I want this too. Us."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Madison - B

Dialogue: "People sleep easier when someone stays awake. I still believe that. But when my watch is over, I know I can finally put the responsibility down and come home to you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Madison - C

Dialogue: "I can't promise I'll always make the right call. I can't promise nothing will ever go wrong. What I can promise is that I'll face whatever comes with you instead of trying to stand between you and the entire world."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "Give me a second. I've got to finish this cut while the measurement's still in my head. All right. What've you got?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "Careful where you stand. I've got lumber stacked behind you, tools to your left, and not much patience for preventable accidents."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "You're catching me in the middle of something. That's normal, by the way. I'm usually in the middle of something."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "You can stay if you want. Just don't move anything. I know exactly where all this is."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "Back again? Fine by me. I've worked around worse distractions."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "There's a stool somewhere under those sketches. You're welcome to it if you can find it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "Hand me that square, would you? No, the other one. Yeah. Thanks."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "If you're going to keep hanging around, you might as well learn where I keep things. It'll save us both time."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "I cleared that corner yesterday. Didn't do it for you specifically, but you do keep standing there, so make use of it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "Here. Hold this end. And don't look so surprised...I wouldn't hand it to you if I thought you'd drop it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "I've got enough work for two people today. Convenient that you showed up."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "You know how to make yourself useful without getting underfoot. That's a rarer skill than it sounds."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "See that joint? Something's bothering me about it. Take a look before I tell you what I think."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "I left a few measurements on the bench. Check them if you're curious. They're not final, so keep the criticism constructive."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "You don't need to ask before using the workbench anymore. Clean up after yourself and we're good."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "I need another pair of hands. Yours, specifically. Everyone else either overthinks my instructions or ignores half of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "Take that side. I'll take this one. We don't need to make a whole discussion out of it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "I was going to do this myself. Then I remembered you're around, and doing it myself suddenly seemed unnecessarily stubborn."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "I left you a list on the bench. Handle whichever part you want. I don't need to check it afterward."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "I started working on your half before realizing it was your half. So I stopped. You're welcome."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "If you think something should be done differently, change it. Just tell me afterward so I know what you did."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "You're late. Not actually...I just got used to you being around earlier than this."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "I made room for your things on the shelf. Figured they'd end up there eventually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "You don't have to help every time you come by, you know. You can just sit there. I'm used to the company."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "Come here. I want to show you something. It's rough, unfinished, and I'm not taking suggestions yet. You're seeing it anyway."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "That's the first sign I ever carved. Crooked letters, bad spacing, terrible finish. I've kept it all these years. Now you know, so don't get sentimental about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "Most people see my work when it's done. You get the warped boards, wrong measurements, bad ideas, and everything else that happens first."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "I left the workshop unlocked for you. If I'm not here, go in. You know what not to touch."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "There's spare gear in the back if you ever need it. Don't bother asking first. That's why I'm telling you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "If something happens and you need me, come find me. Doesn't matter what I'm working on. I'll put it down."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "I caught myself planning tomorrow's work and automatically leaving part of it for you. Didn't even think about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "Used to be, if I couldn't do something alone, I'd assume I'd taken on too much. Now I mostly wonder when you'll be free."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "There's work I'd trust you to finish without me. Never thought I'd say that about anyone outside this shop."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "Here. Catch. That's my spare workshop key. Lose it and I'll be annoyed. Give it back and I'll be more annoyed."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "I don't really think of this as my space when you're here anymore. Doesn't feel like yours either. It's just somewhere we belong."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "I've built plenty of things alone. I'm proud of them too. Still...the things we've done together are the ones I'd rather talk about."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "Your things are everywhere. Mine are everywhere. Somehow I can still find what I need. Guess we've got a system."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "I fixed that loose hinge at home. Didn't touch the other one. You said you'd handle it, so it's yours."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "Still getting used to saying 'our' instead of 'my.' Our tools. Our plans. Our home. Doesn't sound bad."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "I was halfway through fixing something this morning when I realized you'd already started from the other side. We met in the middle. Pretty good summary of us, actually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "You know what's nice? Hearing you working somewhere nearby and not feeling the need to check what you're doing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "I don't keep score anymore. Who did more, who needed help, who leaned on who. Long as we're both standing at the end of it, that's enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Mara - A

Dialogue: "I used to think a strong foundation meant nothing could move it. I was wrong. It means things can shift, settle, take weight, and still hold. That's us."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-mara-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Mara - B

Dialogue: "There's no part of this life I'd rather build alone just to prove I could. I know I could. I'd rather build it with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Mara - C

Dialogue: "Whatever needs fixing, changing, adding, or starting over someday, we'll handle it. Not because either of us needs the other to do it. Because we chose to do it together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-mara-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Hello. Got three repairs left before I can call today productive. Better get back to it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "Busy day. That's all right. Means somebody needs the work."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "If I'm quiet, don't take it personally. Usually means I'm thinking about the next job."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Finished a hoe for Mercer Farms this morning. Axe is next. Then I've got a few hinges waiting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "You keep busy too, from what I've seen. Feels better going to bed tired when you've got something to show for it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Good to see you. Can't talk too long, though. Promised I'd have something finished before supper."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Got through the whole list early today. So I started tomorrow's."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "You've been putting in the hours around Blackmere. People notice. Even when they don't say it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Was wondering if you'd come by. Glad you did. Anyway, I've got iron heating."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Finished earlier than expected. Thought I might go fishing, but Walter needs a repair. Maybe tomorrow."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "There's salmon running upriver. Haven't had time to go after them. Work's work."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "I'd stay and talk awhile, but I've still got orders waiting. Another time."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Was up near Ironveil Peak yesterday. Only for ore. Wouldn't mind going back without a pack full of work."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "Garden's getting overgrown behind the forge. Keep meaning to spend an afternoon on it. Haven't found the afternoon yet."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Good timing. I was about to take a break. Well...I was thinking about taking one. Close enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Took an hour off yesterday. Went fishing. Nothing fell apart while I was gone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "You ever finish everything that needs doing? Starting to think that's not how it works."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "I've got work waiting. It can wait another minute. How've you been?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Closed the forge early yesterday. Didn't have a reason. Just wanted the evening."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "I'm thinking of heading up the mountain this week. No ore. No deliveries. Just a hike."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "I've started keeping one evening free. Still feels like I'm forgetting something."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "There you are. I was hoping I'd see you before I got buried in work."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "Got some time after this. Thought maybe we'd find something to do. If you're free."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "I can finish that order later. Haven't seen you much today."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Had a chance to train under a master smith once. Turned it down. Told myself Blackmere needed me. Still not sure that was the whole reason."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "Never spent much time asking what I wanted. Seemed simpler not to. Doesn't seem as simple anymore."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Sometimes I wonder what I'd do if nobody needed anything from me. Got a few answers now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Going fishing tomorrow. Put it on the schedule and everything. Makes it harder to give the time away."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "Planted the garden again. Not for anybody. Just missed having one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Saved some time for you today. Doesn't matter what we do with it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Said no to an extra job this morning. They found someone else. Felt strange. Also felt good."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "The forge matters to me. Blackmere does too. Doesn't mean they get every hour I've got."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Used to feel guilty whenever I wanted something for myself. Don't as much anymore. Especially when it's time with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Finished everything I meant to finish today. That's enough. Rest of the day's mine."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "I know what I want now. Good work. Good people. Some quiet. And you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "If someone needs me, I'll be there. Same as always. I'm just done pretending I'm not allowed to need a life too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Forge closes on time tonight. I've got plans with my spouse. Sounds good saying that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "Used to work until I ran out of things to do. Now I've got somewhere I'd rather be."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Thought I'd make us dinner tonight. Nothing fancy. Just wanted to."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Took tomorrow off. Figured we could go somewhere. Or stay home. Both sound good."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "Someone asked if I could stay late. Told them no. I've got dinner waiting at home. Never thought I'd be proud of that answer."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Work was good today. Still caught myself looking forward to leaving. That's new."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Marcus - A

Dialogue: "Got work tomorrow. Fishing after. Dinner with you. That's a good life."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-marcus-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Marcus - B

Dialogue: "Spent years making sure everyone else could build a future. Turns out I wanted one too. Glad this is mine."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-marcus-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Marcus - C

Dialogue: "Forge is running. Town's standing. People I care about are doing well. And I get to come home to you. Don't need much more than that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-marcus-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Marcus Rowan

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "There you are! Did I ever tell you about the trout that stole my boot? Meanest fish in the Graythorne. Teeth like a wolf. Probably."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "Perfect timing! I needed an impartial judge. Yesterday's catch was this big. Elise claims it was considerably smaller, but she's tragically committed to facts."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "Careful around me. Spend enough time at the Wharf and you'll either learn to fish or end up in one of my stories. Sometimes both!"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "Ahoy! I've decided you're the mysterious traveler in today's tale. Haven't decided whether you're the hero yet. Try to make a good impression."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "You came back! Excellent. That means either you like talking to me or you've developed a dangerous fascination with fish."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "I've got three stories for you today. One's true, one's mostly true, and one's legally considered slander against a catfish."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "I mentioned you in a story yesterday. Don't worry, I made you sound impressive. Added at least six inches to your height."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "Oh, good! Somebody who already knows my best stories. Now I can start telling you the questionable ones."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "I've figured you out. You're the sort who actually listens when I start rambling. Dangerous habit. Encourages me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "Want to hear something embarrassing? I fell straight into the river this morning. No dramatic rescue. No giant fish involved. Just slipped."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "I've noticed my stories get less impressive when I'm talking to you. Can't get away with anything when somebody remembers the last version."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "You know, you're easy company. I don't have to keep the conversation afloat all by myself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "Had a rotten morning yesterday. There. That's the whole story. No punchline. I'm experimenting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "I started telling everyone this hilarious story earlier, then realized I wasn't really in the mood. Weird feeling, admitting that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "You ever notice how people ask what's wrong the second the funny person gets quiet? Makes being quiet feel like an announcement."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "I went downriver earlier. Got near that quiet stretch and turned around. Usually I'd make up some excuse about the fishing. Not today."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "Some days after the flood were awful. I kept everyone laughing because...well, somebody had to. At least that's what I told myself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "I've got a joke for this part. A good one too. Think I'll leave it alone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "Funny thing about rough water: fighting every wave wears you out faster than riding through some of them. Took me long enough to learn that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "Had a bad day last week. Told Elise. Told you. Didn't cheer either of you up afterward. Somehow everybody survived."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "I'm starting to think people don't need me cheerful every minute. Which is fortunate, because that sounds exhausting now that I've said it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "I went back to that stretch of river. Didn't stay long. Didn't need to. Going there was enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "I always thought stopping to feel something meant I'd get stuck there. Turns out feelings aren't quicksand. Who knew?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "Know what I like about talking to you? I can tell you something sad without watching you wait for me to turn it into something funny."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "Sat by the river for nearly an hour yesterday. Didn't fish. Didn't sing. Didn't even skip a stone. Just sat there. It was nice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "I've told you some stories I don't tell at the Wharf. Funny part is, those might be the ones that sound most like me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "Sometimes I miss who we all were before the flood. There. Said it without pretending there was anything cheerful about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "I've been thinking about all those ridiculous stories I tell. Maybe exaggerating isn't always hiding. Sometimes making people laugh really is just fun."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "Had a wonderful day yesterday. Nothing to prove, nothing to outrun, nothing terrible lurking underneath it. Just a good day."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "You get both versions now, by the way. Nora with the ridiculous fish story and Nora who needs to sit quietly sometimes. No refunds."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "Went back to that part of the river again. Stayed awhile this time. It still hurt. Then I went home. Maybe that's what healing actually looks like."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "I used to think optimism meant finding something good in every bad thing. Now I think sometimes it just means believing the bad thing isn't the end."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "If I'm laughing, you know it's real. If I'm hurting, you know that's real too. Feels good not making you guess."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "Here's today's story: I saw you coming, got happy, and came over to talk. Terrible plot. Great ending."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "I don't need every memory to become a funny story anymore. Some of them can be sad. Some can be beautiful. Some can just be ours."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "You know what? I don't have anything clever today. I'm just really glad you're here."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "Marriage, day whatever-we're-on: spouse remains adorable, household remains afloat, Nora has somehow still not been thrown overboard. Excellent voyage so far."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "I sang all the way home yesterday. Not because I was trying to cheer myself up. I was just happy I was coming home to you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "You know what's strange? I spent years making sure nobody had to sit alone with a bad day. Now when I have one, I know exactly who I want beside me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "We should go down to the river tonight. No fishing gear. No crowd. No grand adventure. Just you, me, and whatever mood we're in."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "Sometimes we sit together without saying anything for ages. Old me would've considered that a conversational emergency. Now it's one of my favorite things."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "I've learned something very important about marriage: if I tell a ridiculous story often enough, my partner can interrupt with the accurate version. Rude, really. But I wouldn't have it any other way."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Nora - A

Dialogue: "I still want a life full of laughter, stories, bad songs, good fishing, and days worth remembering. I just don't need every day to be happy anymore. I only want to live all of them with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-nora-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Nora - B

Dialogue: "Turns out weathering a storm doesn't mean smiling while the rain hits you. Sometimes you complain, sometimes you cry, sometimes you laugh anyway. Then you look beside you and remember you're not out there alone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-nora-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Nora - C

Dialogue: "All right, here's my favorite story. A fisher met someone who made the good days brighter and the bad days easier to bear. She fell completely in love with them. Best part? I don't have to exaggerate a word."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-nora-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Nora Reed

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Hello! How are you settling in? Truly, I mean. There's quite a difference between living somewhere and beginning to feel at home there."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Have you had a chance to meet many people yet? Blackmere can seem rather quiet at first, but there are plenty of kind people here once you know where to find them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Is there anything you've been having trouble with since arriving? You don't have to answer, of course. I simply know that starting somewhere new can be overwhelming."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "You mentioned you were busy the last time we spoke. Has it gotten any easier? I remembered afterward and hoped you weren't pushing yourself too hard."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Have you found somewhere in Blackmere that you enjoy going when you have a little time to yourself? Everyone ought to have somewhere that feels peaceful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "How has your day been? And you're not allowed to say 'fine' merely because it's the easiest answer. I hear enough of those from my students."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Did that problem you told me about work itself out? I've been wondering. I nearly came looking for you yesterday, but I thought that might be a little excessive."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "You seem happier today. Am I right, or am I committing the terrible teacher's habit of trying to read everyone's mood?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Have you been giving yourself any credit for everything you've accomplished lately? Somehow I suspect the answer is no."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Oh! You asked how I was doing. I'm well, thank you. School has been busy, but that's hardly unusual. Now, how are you?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Me? There's nothing particularly interesting to report. One of my students has been struggling with reading, though, and I've been trying something new with them..."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "You really do notice when I change the subject, don't you? That's usually my responsibility."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "All right, since you've asked...I'm tired. Nothing terrible has happened. I've simply been trying to give everyone the attention they deserve, and there never seems to be quite enough of me to go around."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Do you ever worry that you could have done more for someone? I've been thinking about that rather too much lately."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I'm usually quite good at getting people to talk about themselves. You've somehow managed to reverse the process. I'm not certain whether to be impressed or concerned."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Can I ask you something? How do you know when you've done enough? Not everything you possibly could...just...enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "One of my students is having a difficult time. I know struggling is part of learning, but there's still a little voice insisting that a better teacher would know exactly how to help."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I'm not looking for a solution today. I think I only wanted to tell someone what was bothering me. That's allowed, isn't it?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Something lovely happened today. A student who has barely spoken in class volunteered an answer. It wasn't correct, and somehow that made me even prouder. They were willing to try."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "You once told me that progress doesn't have to look impressive to matter. I've been repeating that to myself lately. I think I finally understand what you meant."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Do you know what I celebrated today? One finished page. That's all. A child who's been struggling finished one page and looked enormously proud of themselves. So I decided one page was enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "How am I? Hmm...a little worried, fairly tired, proud of my students, and very happy to see you. There. A complete answer."
