Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "I had a difficult afternoon. I don't particularly want advice, if that's all right. Would you simply keep me company for a little while?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I've discovered something rather embarrassing: being listened to is every bit as comforting as I keep telling everyone else it is."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Would you like to hear about my day? I promise I'm asking because I'd genuinely like to tell you, not because I've forgotten how conversations work."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "I opened my little box of letters again last night. Usually I only do that when I'm doubting myself. This time I wanted to remember all the small things that mattered."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I couldn't help one of my students with something today. I listened, I encouraged them, and then I had to let them make their own choice. I'm trying to believe that was still helping."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "I asked one of my students what they were proud of today. Then they asked me the same question. Apparently children are terribly good at exposing hypocrisy."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "I'm proud of myself today. That felt strangely difficult to say aloud. I helped where I could, accepted where I couldn't, and came home without carrying everyone's problems with me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "You know all those questions I ask you? Whether you've rested, whether you're happy, whether you're being kind to yourself? I've started asking myself some of them too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "A former student visited me today. They remembered something encouraging I'd written on an assignment years ago. I barely remembered writing it. Isn't that extraordinary?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Perhaps we don't always get to know which moments change someone. Maybe our responsibility is simply to offer kindness when we can and trust that it matters."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I used to ask myself whether I was doing enough for everyone. Now I'm trying a different question: did I make someone feel believed in today? That's much easier to answer."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "How are you? I'm asking because I want to know, of course. But when you're finished, I'd like to tell you how I've been too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "You believed in me when I was busy wondering whether I deserved that confidence. I hope you know how much of a difference that made."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I don't need you to solve anything for me. I don't need to solve anything for you either. Sometimes being here, listening, and caring really is enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "How was your day? Mine was lovely, actually. One difficult lesson, two very enthusiastic children, a spilled jar of paint, and the comforting knowledge that I was coming home to you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "I've been looking forward to telling you something all afternoon. Nothing important happened. That's rather the point, I think. I simply wanted to share my day with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I used to think love meant noticing what someone needed before they had to ask. Marriage has taught me another part: trusting them enough to tell you when you need something too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Could we have a quiet evening together tonight? Nothing is wrong. I don't need cheering up. I simply want some time with only you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Do you know one of my favorite questions now? 'What do you want?' Not what someone needs from me, not what would be most useful. Just what I want. Today, the answer is you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Some days I come home proud. Some days I come home wondering whether I did anything right. I love that I can tell you either one without needing to become a different person first."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "I can't promise every student will succeed because of me. I can't promise everyone I love will always be all right. But I can listen, encourage them, believe in them, and love them. I finally think that's enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "For years, I measured how well I cared for people by what I could do for them. Then you taught me something much gentler: sometimes the greatest gift is simply making someone feel that they don't have to face life alone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I have one question left for you. Are you happy? You don't have to say yes every time. Whatever the answer is, I want to be the person you can tell the truth to. You've always been that person for me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I might head north when I leave Blackmere. Or west. There's a trading route along the coast I've been meaning to try. Plenty of time to decide."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "You never know where you'll end up when you stay on the road long enough. That's half the appeal, isn't it? No promises about what comes next."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "If I'm still around next time you come by, remind me to tell you about the markets beyond the eastern ridge. If I'm not...well, I'll tell you next time our paths cross."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Haven't decided how long I'll stay this time. Blackmere's interesting, though. Usually by now I'm already wondering what's over the next hill."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I've got three possible routes marked on my map. Could take any of them tomorrow if the mood struck me. Nice feeling, having options."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Maybe I'll bring you something unusual from my next trip. No guarantees. Half the fun of trading is never knowing what you'll find."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Funny. I've passed the road out of town twice this week and haven't felt particularly tempted to follow it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "There's a caravan leaving soon that I could join. Good route, good company, good trade. Still thinking about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "You keep giving me reasons to stick around another day. Careful. At this rate I'll start thinking Blackmere planned the whole thing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Next time I head out, I think I'll come back through Blackmere. Wasn't originally part of the route, but routes can change."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I've started recognizing people here instead of just remembering customers. That's usually the point where a town gets dangerous."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Could leave tomorrow. Could stay another week. At the moment, staying sounds better. Don't look too pleased with yourself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I saw something on the road last year you'd have loved. Thought about that earlier. Strange, imagining someone in a memory they weren't actually part of."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "There's a place two towns south where the whole market stays open after sunset. Maybe I'll take you there someday. There I go making hypothetical plans again."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Used to be I'd hear about somewhere interesting and immediately start packing. Now I catch myself wondering whether I'll miss anything here."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I've always liked crossroads. Stand in the middle and every direction is still possible. Problem is, eventually you've got to move."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Ever wonder whether choosing the wrong thing is worse than choosing nothing? I've gotten very good at the second one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "I've got a bag packed under my bed. Always have. Makes leaving easy. Lately I've been wondering why that's so important to me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Turned down that caravan route. Good opportunity too. I just...didn't want to go. That's new."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Maybe staying somewhere doesn't mean you've stopped moving. Blackmere certainly hasn't stood still since I arrived."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "I used to count opportunities by how many roads I could take. Starting to think there's another way to measure them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I'm going south next week. Just for a few days. And yes, I'm coming back. See? Didn't even add 'probably.'"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "There's something comforting about traveling when you already know where you're headed afterward. Never thought I'd say that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Next trip, I'm keeping an eye out for something you'd like. That's not a maybe, by the way. Consider it a promise."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I've started unpacking that travel bag. Slowly. Turns out I've been carrying things I haven't needed in years."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I used to leave places before anyone could start expecting me back. Seemed easier that way. I'm not sure easier was better."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Know what I've discovered? Missing someone doesn't ruin a journey. Gives you another reason to finish it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I'm planning a longer trip later this year. Already marked Blackmere as the final stop. First time I've ever planned the return before the departure."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I want to show you some of the places I've seen. Not tell you about them afterward. Actually stand there with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "There are still a thousand roads I want to travel. Good news is, I've stopped thinking I have to travel them alone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I've made a decision. Blackmere's home. There. No qualification, no clever escape clause. Home."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Funny thing about choosing one road: the others don't disappear. They're still there whenever you're ready for another journey."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "I thought belonging somewhere would feel like a door closing. Turns out it feels more like knowing which door you want to walk back through."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Used to ask myself where I might be next year. Now I know where I want to come back to. That's enough certainty for me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I choose you. Sounds almost frighteningly simple after all the years I spent treating choices like traps."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "There'll always be another town, another market, another horizon worth seeing. I still want all of it. I just want you somewhere in the story."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "We're married, and somehow the roads haven't vanished. Very inconsiderate of them after all that worrying I did."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I've been looking at a route along the coast. Thought we could travel it together someday. No rush. We've got a home to come back to."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 13 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "I unpacked the rest of that bag. Kept the bag, obviously. I'm committed, not foolish. A trader should always own good luggage."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-13-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Where should we go someday? North? Coast? Mountains? Pick one. We'll get to the others eventually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I still get restless sometimes. Difference is, now I can say 'let's go somewhere' instead of feeling like I need to disappear."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 14 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "I've started collecting things differently. Used to ask what would remind me of a place. Now I ask what story I'll tell you when I bring it home."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-14-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I spent half my life afraid choosing one future meant losing all the others. Turns out choosing you gave me someone to imagine in every one of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Victor - B

Dialogue: "There are roads I'll take, places I'll miss, opportunities I'll turn down, and others I'll chase halfway across the world. I don't need every possibility anymore. I know which life I want."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 15 - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Come on. We've got an entire world left to see, and for once I'm not worried about choosing where to start. Whichever road we take, we're going together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-15-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "You have a great deal to do and very little experience doing it. An unfortunate combination. Still, experience has to begin somewhere."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "Don't mistake activity for accomplishment. Blackmere has enough unfinished work without you creating more of it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "You're still new enough that people will forgive a few foolish decisions. I suggest you enjoy that privilege while it lasts."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "You've stayed busier than I expected. Whether you've been useful remains a separate question, but you're improving."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "I hear your name rather frequently these days. Mostly attached to good things. Mostly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "You listen when people explain how things are done here. Good. Understanding something before changing it is a habit worth keeping."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "That was sensible, what you did the other day. Don't look so astonished. I give praise when it's warranted."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "You're beginning to understand Blackmere. Not merely its streets and buildings...the people. That's considerably more important."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "I've revised my first impression of you. Slightly upward, before you become too pleased with yourself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "You've made mistakes. Good. Anyone doing worthwhile work eventually does. What interests me is whether you've learned from them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "People have begun relying on you. Take that seriously. Being liked is pleasant...being dependable is useful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "You've earned enough of my respect that I'll tell you when I think you're wrong. Believe me, that is preferable to my deciding you're not worth correcting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "Tell me something. When you look at Blackmere, what do you see that still needs changing? I already know my answer. I'm interested in yours."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "You approach problems differently than my generation did. I haven't decided whether that's wisdom or impatience. Perhaps a little of both."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "I disagreed with one of your decisions recently. Then it worked. An irritating outcome, but an educational one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "Roland and I helped build a very different Blackmere. Sometimes I look around and wonder how much of it ought to remain that way."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "There are traditions in this town older than some of the buildings. I've always considered that a virtue. Lately, I'm beginning to wonder whether I've used age as an argument too often."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "You remind me occasionally that there is more than one sensible way to do something. I would appreciate it if you didn't become insufferable about that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "I've spent decades telling younger people what Blackmere was. Perhaps I ought to spend more time asking what they intend for it to become."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "Suppose you had to choose between preserving an old custom and replacing it with something better. Which would you choose? Think before answering. I certainly would."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "You have ideas I would never have considered at your age. Some are even good. That last part is the troublesome bit."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "I found one of my old journals yesterday. Half the things we considered indispensable when Blackmere was founded disappeared years ago. Somehow, the town survived us."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "Perhaps I've confused remembering the past with repeating it. They're not the same thing, are they? No, don't answer. I know perfectly well they're not."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "I've begun writing about Blackmere differently. Less about what we built, more about why we built it. The distinction matters."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "I'd like your opinion on something I've written. Don't agree with me merely because I'm older. Age deserves consideration, not immunity from criticism."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "If you think one of Blackmere's old ways no longer serves us, say so. I may argue with you. Quite vigorously, in fact. But I'll listen."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "I've noticed I ask what you think rather often now. Evidently you've become someone whose answer I consider worth hearing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "When we founded this town, we made choices our parents would have considered absurd. I seem to have conveniently forgotten that whenever your generation does the same."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "You needn't preserve our answers. Preserve the questions we were trying to answer: How do we care for one another? What do we owe this place? What do we leave behind?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Blackmere will eventually belong to people who remember none of its founders. I'm beginning to think that isn't a tragedy, provided they understand what we stood for."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "You've become part of Blackmere's story, you know. Not because you inherited a manor. Because you've chosen, repeatedly, to invest yourself in the people who live here."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "There are things about Blackmere I would trust you to change now. Do not misunderstand me, that is an extraordinary compliment."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "I spent much of my life helping build this community. It is surprisingly comforting to meet someone I believe will care for it when my generation no longer can."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "I used to wonder whether people like you would remember the Blackmere we built. I ask a different question now: what sort of Blackmere will you build? I look forward to finding out."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "You have my respect. Entirely, not conditionally. Considering how sparingly I distribute it, I suggest you appreciate the significance."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Someday, people will tell stories about the Blackmere you helped shape instead of the one Roland and I helped found. That's as it should be. A legacy isn't something you preserve behind glass. It's something you place in capable hands."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Blackmere still has considerable work ahead of it. Every restored building and reopened path helps. Your efforts are appreciated."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "I've reviewed the latest restoration reports. Progress is steady, though there are several areas requiring attention. There always are."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "If you encounter a problem affecting the community, bring it to Town Hall. I would rather address an issue than discover later that no one reported it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Your work has appeared in several reports recently. Favorably, I should add. Consistency matters more to me than grand gestures."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "People are beginning to recognize your contribution to Blackmere. That carries responsibility. I suspect you're capable of handling it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "We're making progress. Not quickly enough for my preference, perhaps, but progress nonetheless. You've helped with that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I received another good report concerning you this morning. At this point, I may have to accept that you've made yourself useful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "There's something reassuring about knowing certain tasks will be handled without my intervention. You've become one of those assurances."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "You've developed a respectable reputation around town. Protect it. Trust takes considerably longer to rebuild than a damaged wall."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I have two proposals on my desk regarding the same problem. Both have merit. I've been considering which one you would favor."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "You've spoken with people I don't always hear from directly. If you notice concerns being overlooked, I expect you to tell me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "I'm interested in your perspective on Blackmere's recovery. Not as mayor addressing a resident. Consider this an unofficial conversation."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "There are mornings when every decision seems to create three more decisions. Today has been one of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "I changed my mind about something today. Publicly, even. The town survived the spectacle."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "People tend to bring me problems once they've already decided I should know the solution. It's refreshing speaking with someone who permits me to think first."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I approved an early evacuation during the Flood. Then redirected resources when another district was hit. I can still list every reason for those decisions...and every person we couldn't reach."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "Everyone tells me we did everything we could during the Flood. I've never found that particularly comforting. Everything we could still wasn't enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "There are decisions from that day I have reconsidered hundreds of times. Different routes. Different priorities. Different outcomes. None of the calculations change what happened."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I caught myself reviewing old Flood reports again last night. At some point, studying the past stops being preparation and becomes punishment."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "Madison once told me that no amount of reviewing a decision can give me information I didn't have when I made it. Irritatingly sensible woman."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "I've spent so long asking what I should have done differently that I occasionally neglect a more useful question: what can I do now?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I walked through Town Square before sunrise this morning. For once, I didn't make a list of everything that still needs repairing. I simply looked at what we've already rebuilt."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "There's laughter outside Town Hall again. Children running through the square. Shops opening. Ordinary things. I underestimated how extraordinary ordinary could feel."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "I used to measure recovery by what we'd restored. Buildings. Roads. Services. Lately, I find myself measuring it by how often people make plans for tomorrow."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I went to Memorial Hill recently. I usually leave feeling as though I've renewed a debt. This time...I wondered whether the people we lost would truly want that from me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "I've always believed honoring the dead meant remembering everything we failed to save. Perhaps it also means protecting the future they should have had."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "I cannot change who we lost. Saying that aloud once felt like surrender. Now I think accepting it may be the only way to properly serve everyone still here."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I've begun keeping tomorrow's plans on top of the old recovery reports instead of beneath them. A trivial change, perhaps. It doesn't feel trivial."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "Blackmere does not need a mayor who can undo every mistake. It needs one willing to make the next decision with courage. I believe I can do that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "For the first time in a long while, I'm excited about where this town is going. Not relieved. Not cautiously optimistic. Excited."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I used to think leadership meant carrying whatever everyone else could not. I understand now why that nearly crushed me. A community is stronger when everyone carries a little of the weight."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "You've done more than help rebuild Blackmere. You've reminded people that rebuilding is worth doing. A mayor cannot manufacture that kind of hope."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "I trust your judgment. Not because I expect you to be right every time, but because I've seen what you do when you aren't. That's the more valuable quality."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "No reports today. No restoration figures either. I'm simply glad to see you. There, I've managed an entire greeting without conducting municipal business."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "When I look at Blackmere now, I don't see a town I failed to protect. I see people who endured something terrible and chose to build again. I'm proud to be one of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "You've become someone I value well beyond what you've done for this town. I hope you know that. Consider it a statement from Beatrice rather than the mayor."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Long shift today. Plenty of ore to move. Better than standing around wishing there was work."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "If something needs carrying, carry it. If it needs fixing, fix it. Most problems get simpler once you stop staring at them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "I've got work waiting at the mine and more waiting at home. That's life. No sense complaining about a full day."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "You work hard. I've noticed. Don't need to make a show of it either. I respect that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "There's always another load after the one you're carrying. Trick is learning not to waste strength worrying about the next one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Jonas says I take on too much. I say everything gets done. We have this conversation often."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Had a support beam give me trouble earlier. Heavy thing. Marcus offered to help. Told him I had it. I did."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Jasmine wanted to take over one of my chores yesterday. Told her not to worry about it. She's got enough of her own."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "You don't have to offer me a hand every time something looks heavy. Been doing this a long time."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "You asked why I work so much. Simple answer: my family's counting on me. That's reason enough."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "I grew up knowing security doesn't happen by accident. You build it. Day by day, shift by shift."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Nothing wrong with being tired when the people you love have what they need. That's a fair trade."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Jonas got irritated with me yesterday. Said I came home exhausted, then started repairing the fence. Apparently sitting down was also an option."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Milo tried carrying a basket for me. Nearly dragged it along the ground. Wouldn't let go, though. Stubborn boy. No idea where he gets that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "You and Jonas have something in common. Both of you give me that same look when I say I'm not tired."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Pulled something in my shoulder at the mine. Nothing serious. Jonas made me leave the woodpile alone when I got home. Wasn't pleased about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Kira told me I can't teach her to be capable if I never let her do anything difficult. Hard to argue when your own child catches you contradicting yourself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "You ever notice how easy it is to tell someone else they don't have to do everything alone? Seems the advice gets less convenient when it's yours."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Let Jonas finish a job without stepping in today. He did it differently than I would've. Still got done."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Kira handled the garden this morning. I checked it once. All right, twice. Didn't change anything."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Milo carried the vegetables inside yesterday. Took him three trips. I could've done it in one. Kept my mouth shut."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Had more ore than I could move alone today. Asked for another set of hands before my back made the decision for me. Progress, apparently."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Jonas asked what needed doing tonight. Usually I say nothing. This time I gave him half the list. Should've seen his face."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Turns out asking for help takes less time than insisting you don't need it. Wish I'd worked that out twenty years ago."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Something you said stuck with me. If I never let my family carry anything for me, I'm not protecting them. I'm deciding what they're capable of."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "I've always wanted Kira and Milo to know they can depend on us. Didn't think enough about whether I was showing them that I can depend on them too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Jonas has spent years standing beside me. Suppose I got so focused on being the foundation that I forgot foundations aren't made from one stone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Came home tired yesterday and said so. Jonas cooked. Kira finished the garden. Milo brought me a blanket I didn't need. I let him anyway."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "I've got people willing to take some weight when mine gets heavy. Took me long enough to understand that's something to be grateful for, not ashamed of."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Funny thing. The farm didn't fall apart because I rested for an evening. Nobody went hungry either. Jonas has requested I remember this evidence."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "I used to think providing for my family meant making sure they never had to carry what I carried. Maybe it means teaching them we carry things together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Kira's stronger than I give her credit for. Milo will be too. Jonas always has been. Doesn't diminish me any to admit that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Strength isn't doing everything yourself. Took a miner far too long to learn something that obvious about load-bearing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Had a hard day. I'm tired, my back hurts, and there's work left for tomorrow. That's all right. I've got people waiting at home who'll carry their share."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "You know, I don't worry quite as much about what happens if I can't manage everything someday. Jonas, Kira, Milo, Jasmine...we'll manage it together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "You've become a good friend. One of the few I'd admit that to without immediately finding something heavy to move. Don't expect me to get sentimental about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "I trust you slept adequately, [Player]. There is much to be done at Pendrelle Manor, but I assure you, there is no virtue in beginning an already demanding day exhausted."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "Should you require anything concerning the manor, you need only ask. Your grandmother entrusted its care to me for many years. I intend to extend that same standard of service to you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor has been quiet for far too long, [Player]. Your arrival has already changed that considerably. I believe your grandmother would have been pleased."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "You are beginning to establish a routine here. I have taken the liberty of adjusting mine accordingly. A household functions best when its rhythms complement those who live within it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "I noticed you returned rather late yesterday. There is no need for concern, I merely kept supper warm somewhat longer than usual. Such things are easily accommodated."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Your grandmother had certain habits I could predict down to the minute. You, I am discovering, are rather less predictable. It keeps an old butler attentive."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "I have begun recognizing which rooms you favor, when you prefer quiet, and when a cup of tea is best provided without discussion. Observation is an indispensable part of good service."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "You needn't thank me each time I attend to something around the manor. Though I confess, your doing so is...appreciated, [Player]."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "There are fewer empty rooms these days, somehow. Not literally, of course. The manor simply feels occupied again. There is a meaningful distinction."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "You asked whether I ever tire of looking after this place. No, [Player]. There are duties one performs because one must, and others because performing them gives one's days purpose. This has always been the latter."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "Myrtle once accused me of treating rest as though it were a clerical error. I informed her that was absurd. She remained entirely unconvinced."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "I appreciate your concern, but there is no need to worry after me. I have spent a lifetime caring for others. I assure you, I am quite accustomed to it, [Player]."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "You have developed an unfortunate habit of asking whether I have eaten. That is ordinarily my question to you. I find the reversal rather disorienting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "I found tea waiting for me this afternoon. I presume that was your doing. Entirely unnecessary...and very thoughtful. Thank you, [Player]."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "You need not concern yourself with whether I am comfortable. Although I am beginning to suspect that telling you so only encourages you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "I remained at Pendrelle Manor because I made your grandmother a promise. For many years, that explanation was sufficient. Recently, I find it no longer explains everything."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "There was a time after Myrtle passed when maintaining the manor felt less like tending a home and more like preserving a memory. I did not realize how lonely that had become until you arrived, [Player]."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "I had expected your arrival to give me new responsibilities. I had not expected to become accustomed to your company quite so quickly. One cannot prepare for everything, it seems."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "Martin informed me yesterday that I speak of you with considerable pride. I told him he was imagining things. Unfortunately, my son knows me rather too well."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "I have served this family for most of my life. Forgive me, that phrasing is becoming inaccurate. I have served your grandmother and now you. The family itself has given me considerably more than employment."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "You asked what I wanted for the manor, [Player]. I nearly gave you the usual answer: restoration, stability, proper stewardship. The truth is simpler. I should like it to feel happy again."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "I caught myself referring to Pendrelle Manor as 'our home' this morning. An imprecise choice of words, technically speaking. I have decided not to correct it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "There are responsibilities I once considered mine alone. I am learning that protecting a legacy does not require guarding every part of it from everyone else."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "You have become someone whose judgment I trust deeply, [Player]. Given certain responsibilities I have carried for a very long time, I hope you understand the significance of that statement."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "Your grandmother once told me that I had an extraordinary talent for standing inside a family while insisting I remained outside it. At the time, I considered the remark unfair."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "I believed professional distance made my loyalty purer somehow. Looking back, that was a peculiar notion. Affection never made me less devoted to Myrtle. I cannot imagine why I assumed it would with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "When you are away from the manor longer than expected, I worry. I could call that professional concern, certainly. We both know by now that would not be the entire truth, [Player]."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "I have spent so many years anticipating what others require that accepting care myself remains rather difficult. I am trying, [Player]. You have been remarkably patient with me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "You told me to leave the silver until tomorrow and sit down for tea with you. I confess, my first instinct was horror. My second was that I rather liked the idea."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "There is something profoundly comforting about being wanted at a table for one's company rather than one's ability to set it properly. I had forgotten that, [Player]."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "Myrtle asked me to protect this manor and the person who inherited it. I intend to honor that promise for as long as I am able. But my concern for you ceased being merely an obligation some time ago."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "I used to believe there ought to be a clear boundary between service and affection. Life has been kind enough to demonstrate how thoroughly mistaken I was."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "You are perfectly capable of managing without me someday. I hope you know that, [Player]. Strangely, realizing it no longer frightens me quite as much. Perhaps being needed was never the same as belonging."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Edward - A

Dialogue: "For most of my life, I have called Pendrelle Manor my place of service. I think I am finally old enough to admit what everyone else apparently understood years ago. It is my home."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Edward - B

Dialogue: "Your grandmother was family to me, though I rarely permitted myself to say so. You are as well, [Player]. I hope you will forgive an old butler for taking several decades to become comfortable with the terminology."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Whatever becomes of Pendrelle Manor in the years ahead, I no longer believe Myrtle's legacy resides in its walls, its furnishings, or even the duties she entrusted to me. It lives in the family that fills this home with life. I am deeply honored to count myself among them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "You seem determined to establish yourself here. Determination is useful. Whether it produces results remains to be seen."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Blackmere is rebuilding under difficult circumstances. Anyone contributing to that effort is worth observing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "First impressions are unreliable. Consistent behavior is considerably more informative. I prefer to wait."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "You've been dependable thus far. A limited sample, admittedly, but a favorable one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "People have begun speaking well of you. I place more value on repeated observations than reputation, but in this case, the two appear consistent."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "You've followed through on what you've said you would do. That is rarer than it should be."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "If I were assessing your reliability formally, your position would have improved. Fortunately, friendship does not require paperwork."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "I expected your enthusiasm to diminish once Blackmere stopped being new to you. It hasn't. I'll admit, I miscalculated."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "Your decisions are occasionally unconventional. They have also been more sensible than I initially anticipated. Both observations are true."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "I find myself less inclined to question your intentions lately. Your methods, perhaps. But not your intentions."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "You've established a respectable pattern: you listen, consider the problem, then act. I appreciate predictable competence."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "I've started assuming you'll keep your word. Ordinarily, I dislike assumptions. This one has proven surprisingly safe."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "Nora says I evaluate people as though they're financial decisions. She's exaggerating. Slightly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Trust has always seemed straightforward to me. Observe someone long enough, establish a pattern, assess the risk. I'm beginning to suspect that's an incomplete definition."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "You've become someone I expect to see regularly. I hadn't realized that until you were absent yesterday."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "I make decisions affecting people's futures every day. One approval can help someone rebuild. One mistake can leave them worse off. Caution seems entirely reasonable under those circumstances."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Numbers are reassuring. They don't become offended, conceal their intentions, or change their minds without warning. People are considerably more complicated."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "I can calculate whether a business is likely to succeed. I cannot calculate whether someone will disappoint me. I've always disliked that distinction."

Event: Generic
