ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "I've been attempting to determine precisely when I began trusting you. There doesn't appear to be a single point. Very inconvenient."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "You've made choices I wouldn't have recommended. Some failed. Some succeeded. Oddly, neither outcome changed my opinion of you very much."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "Apparently, trusting someone does not require believing they'll always make the correct decision. That should have been obvious."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "I told you something recently without first considering every possible consequence of telling you. I noticed afterward. Nothing disastrous occurred."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "There's a peculiar comfort in knowing I don't have to present you with a perfectly reasoned version of every thought."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "I had a difficult day yesterday. Normally, I would leave the statement there. With you, I find myself wanting to explain why."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "There are people in Blackmere whose payments I've quietly delayed when circumstances demanded it. It isn't something I discuss. I'm not entirely certain why I'm telling you now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "I've covered shortages myself before. Small amounts. Enough to keep someone stable. The figures rarely justified it. I did it anyway."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "Perhaps I've always understood that not every worthwhile decision can be defended on a ledger. I simply preferred not to examine the contradiction."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "I approved something today with less certainty than I usually require. The preparation was sound. The person was trustworthy. Eventually, I had to accept that certainty wasn't coming."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Preparation reduces risk. It does not eliminate it. I'm beginning to think I've spent years confusing those two goals."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "There are decisions worth making even when the outcome isn't guaranteed. I still dislike that fact. I no longer dispute it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "If you asked whether trusting you is objectively safe, I couldn't prove it. I find that bothers me considerably less than it once would have."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "You've become one of the people I rely upon. No analysis attached. No qualifications. I simply do."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "I used to think vulnerability meant accepting unnecessary risk. Now I think refusing every risk carries its own cost."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Elise - A

Dialogue: "I've considered the matter carefully. You're my friend. There is no practical reason I needed to phrase that as a conclusion, but you know me well enough to expect one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Some investments provide security. Others provide opportunity. Friendship seems to offer neither guarantee, yet somehow returns something more valuable."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Elise - C

Dialogue: "I still believe security is built through careful decisions. I've merely added one consideration: sometimes the careful decision is trusting someone when the numbers cannot tell you what will happen."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "There you are! Come in, come in. If you've been running around town all morning, sit down for a minute before you start on whatever's next. People forget that resting is part of getting things done."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Have you eaten? That's not me trying to sell you something, by the way. I ask everyone. Helen says I could meet someone in the middle of a burning building and still wonder whether they'd had breakfast."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "You're always welcome here. Doesn't matter whether you need something or just want somewhere warm to sit for a while. An inn ought to have room for both."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Good to see you again! I was starting to recognize your footsteps. Or maybe that's just what happens when you spend enough years listening for guests coming through the door."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "You look like you've had a full day. If it was a good one, you can tell me about it. If it wasn't, you can still tell me about it. I'm not particular."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "I've noticed you don't need much fussing over. Doesn't mean I'll stop offering, mind you. Hospitality isn't something I switch off just because someone can take care of themselves."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Ah, perfect timing! I was just thinking I hadn't seen you today. Not that I'm keeping track. Well...I suppose technically I am now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "I've figured something out about you: when you say you'll help someone, you usually mean it. That's the sort of thing people remember, even if they don't always say so."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "You know, you're getting dangerously close to becoming a regular. Next thing you know, I'll remember what you like without asking. Then there's no going back."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "I saved you a seat earlier without thinking about it. Someone nearly took it, too. I almost told them it was reserved before realizing I'd never actually asked whether you were coming."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Funny how quickly people become part of the rhythm around here. One day they're a new face at the counter, then suddenly you notice when their usual chair is empty."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "Helen caught me wondering whether you'd stop by today. She gave me that little smile of hers and said, 'Sounds like you've made another friend.' Hard woman to argue with."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Busy morning! Breakfast crowd, two rooms needing attention, Felix misplaced something important that turned out to be in his pocket, and I haven't sat down since sunrise. Good day, though. Productive."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Helen says I have a habit of finding one more thing to do whenever I'm supposed to be finished. She's right, of course. Don't tell her I admitted that without an argument."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "You don't have to ask whether I'm tired every time I look tired. I'm an innkeeper. Looking tired is practically part of the uniform! ...All right, yes. I'm a little tired."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "We had a guest leave unhappy once, years ago. Nothing terrible, just a meal that took too long and a room that wasn't quite right. I still remember their face. Ridiculous, isn't it? Hundreds of happy guests, and that's the one that sticks."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Sometimes I wonder what would happen if I couldn't keep up with all this anymore. The inn, the guests, making sure everyone has what they need...then I usually decide there's work to do and stop wondering."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "I know I can't make every person who walks through that door happy. Sensible part of me understands that perfectly. The rest of me apparently missed the meeting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "You know what's strange? I spend all day telling travelers to slow down, eat something warm, get some sleep, take care of themselves. Apparently I've never considered myself eligible for the same advice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Edward once told me that being dependable doesn't mean making yourself indispensable. I nodded like that was very wise, then proceeded to ignore it for years."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "Helen made me take an afternoon off. A whole afternoon! I spent the first hour wondering what was happening here, the second thinking of things I should be doing, and the third...well, the third was actually rather nice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "I let someone else handle the counter for a while yesterday. Nothing caught fire, every guest survived, and the inn was still standing when I came back. Very humbling experience."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "You offered to help me the other day, and my first thought was to tell you I had everything handled. Then I realized I was carrying three things at once. So...perhaps you had a point."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "I've always thought caring for people meant taking something off their plate. Turns out sometimes they genuinely want to take something off mine. I'm still getting used to that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Sat by the river for a while yesterday. Didn't fish, didn't plan tonight's supper, didn't make a list in my head. Just sat there. Felt odd for about five minutes. Then it felt wonderful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "I've been trying something new: when Helen asks whether I need help, I answer honestly. You'd think after all these years of marriage I'd have discovered that sooner."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "I think I worried that if I stopped doing everything, people might stop needing this place. Silly when you say it aloud. They don't come because I never sit down. They come because they feel at home."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "I closed up a little early the other night. Sat with Helen, heard all about Felix's latest grand adventure, and didn't once get up to wipe down the counter. Well...I thought about it twice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Something finally occurred to me: if I run myself into the ground making sure everyone has somewhere to return to, eventually I won't be here when they return. Doesn't sound very hospitable when I put it that way."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "You know you're allowed behind the counter if I'm overwhelmed. Not because you're a guest I'm putting to work! Because you're a friend, and I've finally learned that friends are going to help whether I make it easy for them or not."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "I used to think the inn felt welcoming because I worked hard enough to make it that way. That's part of it, sure. But Helen, Felix, Theo, everyone who gathers here...they're what gives the place its warmth. I just help keep the door open."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "There's always room for one more. I've said that for years. Funny thing is, I never realized it applied to letting one more person share the work, too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "I took an evening for myself this week. Nobody was disappointed in me. Nobody felt unwelcome. And the next morning, I actually had more energy to give everyone. Helen has been remarkably gracious about not saying 'I told you so.' Much."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "You don't need an invitation anymore, you know. If the door's open, come in. If it's closed, knock. You're not just another guest passing through. You're one of the people who makes this place feel like home."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "I've spent years trying to make sure everyone who walks through this door knows there's a place for them here. You've helped me realize something rather important: there's a place for me here too, even when I'm not serving anybody."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "All right, sit down. I'll sit too. No polishing glasses, no checking the kitchen, no finding some mysterious task that absolutely cannot wait. We can just talk for a while. That's what friends do, isn't it? And before you answer, yes, I've already eaten."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "You look hungry. Don't argue, I spend enough time feeding people to recognize that look. I've got something warm in the kitchen that'll fix you right up."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I was trying a new bread recipe this morning and made far too much. Frederick claims I do that on purpose so I'll have an excuse to feed whoever wanders in. He's probably right."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "If you've had a difficult day, you're in the right place. Sit somewhere comfortable, have something to eat, and give the world a little time to become manageable again."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I'm still figuring out what to make when you come by. Everyone has a dish that makes their shoulders drop the moment they smell it. I'll discover yours eventually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "You liked what I made last time, didn't you? I thought so! I wrote down what I changed before I forgot. Nothing worse than accidentally making something wonderful and never managing it again."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I nearly put a plate aside for you earlier. Then I thought, Helen, you can't start preparing meals for people who haven't even said they're coming. Give me another week."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "There you are! I saved you a little of today's special. You've officially reached the point where I think about whether you've eaten before you've even walked through the door."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I've decided what I'd cook if you came in absolutely miserable. No, I'm not telling you. Hopefully we'll never need to test whether I've chosen correctly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "You know what I like about feeding people? For a little while, everyone has permission to stop. You sit, you eat, you talk. Nothing else needs you until the plate is empty."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I've started a page for you in my recipe notebook. Nothing elaborate! Just little notes about what you've enjoyed. I like remembering those things."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "Some recipes become attached to people without you meaning them to. I can't make Frederick's favorite without thinking about our first years at the inn. I wonder what I'll eventually associate with you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "My cookbook is becoming less of a cookbook and more of a history book with ingredient lists. Birthdays, celebrations, ridiculous accidents...apparently food remembers people rather well."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I added a note beside one of your recipes today. Not about the food, but about something you said that made me laugh. That's how it starts. Eventually there'll be more story than recipe."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "Theo teases me about that notebook. Says someday Felix will inherit six hundred pages explaining who ate what and why it mattered. I told him that sounds like valuable family history."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I think that's why I love cooking for people. You get to preserve a moment without trapping it. Make the same dish years later, and suddenly everyone you shared it with is sitting beside you again."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I ruined a batch of bread this morning Theo had started. Forgot it was proofing because I was preparing three other things at once. Then I started another batch immediately, which Frederick suggested was perhaps missing the lesson."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "You know how I can usually tell what someone needs to eat just by looking at them? Lately Frederick has been giving me that same look and telling me what I need is a chair."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I was so tired last night I nearly put salt into the preserves instead of sugar. I laughed about it afterward, but...well, perhaps being that tired isn't particularly funny."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "Frederick made supper yesterday. I kept wandering into the kitchen asking whether he needed anything until he threatened to lock me out. So I sat with Felix instead. It was lovely, actually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "Someone cooked for me today. Do you know how difficult it is to sit still while another person works in your kitchen? I nearly wore a path into the floor."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I always thought accepting a meal was the easy side of cooking. Apparently not. When you're used to being the one serving, letting someone place a plate in front of you feels surprisingly vulnerable."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I left the kitchen before everything was finished yesterday. Frederick handled the rest. The food was wonderful, the guests were happy, and absolutely no one stood up to announce that I'd failed them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I've spent years saying that meals taste better when everyone contributes something. I'm beginning to realize I somehow exempted myself from the 'everyone' part."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "You were right to tell me to slow down. I still don't particularly enjoy admitting that. Perhaps I'll bake you something as thanks...No, wait. That rather defeats the point, doesn't it?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I wrote down a recipe yesterday that I didn't cook. Just the idea, then I closed the notebook and went for a walk. Do you know how unusual that is for me?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "Felix asked me to sit with him after supper. My first thought was everything I still needed to clean. Then I remembered he'll only be this age once. The dishes survived until morning."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I've always written down the meals people remember. I'm starting to write down the quiet moments too. Tea with Frederick. An afternoon with Felix. Watching dough rise with my brother. A conversation with a good friend. Those belong in the book."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I was tired this morning, so I asked for help before I became exhausted. Imagine that! No ruined bread, no forgotten ingredients, no heroic collapse over a saucepan. Very efficient."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I've started leaving one evening a week without planning anything for anyone. Frederick laughed when I called that a schedule, but if I don't schedule the rest, I'll fill it with work."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I used to worry that stepping away meant someone might need me while I was gone. Now I remind myself that being surrounded by capable people is something to celebrate, not something to fear."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I changed one of my oldest recipes recently. Used to think that was almost disrespectful. Then I remembered recipes are supposed to keep feeding people, not remain frozen forever. Perhaps people aren't so different."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "A warm meal can heal more than an empty stomach. I've believed that my whole life. What I forgot was that sometimes I'm the one who needs to sit at the table."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "There will always be another meal to prepare and another person I'd like to care for. I'm finally learning that resting doesn't take anything away from them. It means I'll still have something left to give tomorrow."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Helen - A

Dialogue: "Your page in my cookbook has gotten rather crowded. Meals you've loved, things you've said, days I want to remember...I suppose that's what happens when someone stops being a guest and becomes part of the story."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I hope you know you never need a reason to come sit at our table. Hungry, happy, exhausted, lonely, celebrating, or simply passing by, you've become family around here. There's always a place for you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I made tea, and that's all I'm making right now. Come sit with me. Frederick has the inn, Felix is happily occupied, and the kitchen can survive with just Theo for a while. I'd rather spend the time with a good friend."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "You're early. I approve."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "The bell keeps its schedule. I recommend doing the same."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Blackmere has its rhythms. You'll learn them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "You've been here often lately. I've noticed."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "You seem to keep your commitments. That's worth remembering."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "I expected you around this time. Apparently you've become predictable."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "Late today. Only slightly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "I heard you before I saw you. I'm beginning to recognize your footsteps."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "You've settled into Blackmere well. Better than some."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "I adjusted my morning schedule yesterday. Five minutes. Nothing collapsed."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "You interrupted my routine the other day. Strangely, I didn't mind."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Routine is useful. It needn't become law."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "James believed punctuality was a virtue. He was frequently late."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "This watch was his. I still wind it every morning."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "You've heard me mention James before. I don't do that with everyone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "James rang that bell before me. Sometimes I still hear his rhythm in mine."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "I inherited his duties. Somewhere along the way, I made them my life."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Changing the tower once felt disrespectful. Even small things. I've begun questioning that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "I moved one of James's tools yesterday. It had been in the same place for decades. Irrational, perhaps. Difficult nonetheless."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Memory is peculiar. I can change a room without changing what happened inside it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "I've preserved many things because James knew them. I'm considering which ones I preserve because I value them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "I tried something different at the tower today. Agatha will be insufferably pleased."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Traditions change. Slowly, preferably. But they change."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "I once thought preserving something meant keeping it unchanged. I was mistaken."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "I spoke to James last night. Told him about something new for once."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "I still talk to him after the final bell. I imagine you've guessed that by now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Remembering James isn't painful every time anymore. Sometimes it's simply...good."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "I bought something new for my home. No practical reason. I merely liked it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "I've begun making plans farther ahead. Plans for myself, I mean."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Tomorrow doesn't require permission from yesterday. Took me long enough to understand that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "The tower has changed. So have I. James remains part of both."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Every bell marks something ending. It also marks something beginning. I neglected the second half for many years."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "I no longer fear change quite as I did. Don't expect enthusiasm. Tolerance is sufficient progress."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Irene - A

Dialogue: "You've become part of my routine. One of the changes I intend to keep."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Irene - B

Dialogue: "James belongs to my yesterday. You and many others belong to my today. There is room for both."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Every moment matters because none of them return. I've finally realized that's a reason to welcome the next one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Oh! There you are! Come here, come here...I was just telling Kira that I hoped I'd see you today. How are you? Have you eaten? Has anyone done anything nice for you yet? Because if the answer to that last one is no, we're fixing it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "You know what I've decided? Nobody should leave Mercer Farms in a worse mood than when they arrived. So if you're having a good day, wonderful! If you're having a terrible one, well...give me a few minutes."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Hi! Before you say anything, yes, I know I'm smiling at you like we've known each other for years. I do that. Makes skipping all the awkward stranger business much easier."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "I remembered something you told me last time! See? You thought you were just chatting, but I was listening. I always listen. Well, except when I'm talking, which admittedly happens quite a lot."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "How's everything going? And don't give me the polite answer unless the polite answer happens to be true. I've got time for the long version."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "You're back! Excellent. I was worried all my talking might've frightened you off. Jonas says that's impossible because anyone who survives one conversation with me knows exactly what they're getting into."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Okay, I think I've figured you out a little. Not completely! Where would be the fun in that? But enough that I can usually tell whether you're actually doing well or just saying you are."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I saw something earlier that made me think of you. Then I realized that's been happening surprisingly often lately. Congratulations, you've apparently moved into a little corner of my brain."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "You know what? I like when you stop by. There! Nice and simple. No story attached, no dramatic explanation. Aren't you proud of me?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "I've officially started remembering your little things. The things you like, the things you don't, what makes you laugh...that sort of thing. That's when you know you're in trouble with me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "Kira asked why I knew something about you yesterday. I said because we're friends. Then I stopped and thought, oh! We are, aren't we? That was nice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "If you ever need company, come find me. Happy company, sad company, quiet company...although I should warn you I'm still practicing that last one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Someone was upset earlier, so naturally I brought them food, told three ridiculous stories, found someone they hadn't seen in ages, and nearly organized an entire picnic before they convinced me they just wanted to sit quietly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "Do you ever get that feeling when someone you care about is unhappy and suddenly you can't think about anything else until you've fixed it? No? Hmm. Maybe that's a me problem."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Claudia told me I don't have to solve everyone's problems. I told her I know that. Then she just looked at me. You know the look. The one that means she knows you're lying."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "I'm fine! Really. Just tired. Well...and a little overwhelmed. But mostly fine! See, this is why I prefer asking how you're doing. Much easier conversation."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "You caught me on a strange day. Nothing terrible happened. I just don't feel particularly cheerful. I keep waiting for myself to snap out of it, which apparently isn't helping."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "I know what you're doing. You're checking on me. Very sneaky. That's supposed to be my job, you know...Thank you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "I tried telling someone I wasn't having a good day yesterday. Do you know what they did? They listened. That's it! No panic, no disappointment, no sudden collapse of community morale. Imagine that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I think I've spent so long being the person who cheers everyone up that I forgot people might want to be there when I'm not cheerful. Seems obvious when I say it aloud."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Sometimes people are sad and there's nothing you can do about it. I hate that sentence. Absolutely hate it. I'm beginning to think it might still be true."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Milo was upset yesterday. I almost launched into my usual whole production, but instead I sat beside him. Eventually he leaned against me. Didn't say much. Didn't need to."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I've been experimenting with not immediately fixing things. It's awful. You just sit there caring about someone while their problem continues existing! Apparently that's called being supportive."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Someone doesn't always need a joke, or food, or a gathering, or twelve enthusiastic suggestions. Sometimes they need someone who isn't frightened by their sadness. I'm trying to become better at that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "I have a little journal where I write down birthdays and favorite foods and important dates. There. Secret's out. My legendary memory has been fraudulent this entire time."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "Your name has quite a few notes beside it in my journal now. Don't look so worried! They're nice things. Mostly. There may be one extremely embarrassing story I'm preserving for historical purposes."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "I started that journal because I never wanted anyone I cared about to feel forgotten. Lately I've realized people can know you remember them even if you miss a birthday or forget their favorite pie."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "I'm having a bad day. There. No cheerful ending attached. No reassurance that I'll be fine in five minutes. Just a bad day. ...Would you mind staying for a bit?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I said no to organizing something this week because I was tired. Someone else volunteered, everyone had a wonderful time, and Blackmere somehow continued functioning without my personal supervision."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "I've started asking myself a new question when someone is hurting: 'Do they need my help, or do they need me?' Turns out those are very different things."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Someone I love was unhappy yesterday. I couldn't fix it. And you know what? We sat together while bread was in the over, talked when they wanted to talk, stayed quiet when they didn't...and I think I actually helped more than I usually do."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I've spent years trying to make Mercer Farms the place where nobody feels alone. I still want that. I just understand now that people don't have to be smiling to know they belong."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "You know me well enough now that I don't feel like I have to brighten the room the second you arrive. Which is funny, because seeing you usually brightens it anyway."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "You're family around here now. Not 'come to every gathering because Jasmine will hunt you down if you don't' family. Real family. The kind where you can show up happy, miserable, exhausted, excited, or saying nothing at all."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I used to think caring about someone meant doing everything possible to make them happy. Now I think it's simpler than that. You stay. You listen. You love them through whatever they're feeling. You've helped teach me that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Oh, good, it's you. I don't have a funny story today, I haven't baked anything, and I'm not planning a single gathering. I thought maybe we could just sit together for a while. Look at that, I'm finally learning."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Welcome. Fields are coming along nicely. Takes time, mostly. Good soil, steady work, and enough patience not to meddle with things every five minutes."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Good to see you. Seems like you're finding your footing around Blackmere. Just keep at it. Most things settle into place eventually."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "You've been keeping busy. That's good. Don't worry if everything isn't working yet. Nothing worthwhile grows overnight."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "There you are. I was wondering if I'd see you today. Guess you're becoming part of the usual scenery around here."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "You're good about lending a hand without making people ask twice. Folks notice that sort of thing. I do too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Farm teaches you pretty quickly who shows up when there's work to do. You've been showing up."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Had a stubborn fence post this morning. Could've fought with it for another hour, but I stepped away and came back later. Sometimes giving a problem room is the best thing you can do."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Kira and Milo were arguing earlier. I figured they'd sort it out themselves. Sure enough, ten minutes later they were laughing again. Usually better not to interfere too quickly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "People aren't that different from crops sometimes. Push too hard and you can do more harm than good. Give them patience, though, and you'd be surprised what works itself out."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "You know, talking with you is easy. Don't feel like I need to fill every quiet patch. That's rarer than you'd think."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "I've gotten used to seeing you around. Farm feels a little quieter when you haven't stopped by in a while."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "You're welcome here anytime. No need to find an excuse. If I'm working, walk with me. If I'm resting, pull up a chair."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Claudia and I disagreed about something yesterday. Nothing serious. I let it go. No sense turning a little difference into a bigger one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Jasmine says I avoid arguments. I prefer to think I don't go looking for them. She says that's exactly what someone who avoids arguments would say."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Peace at home matters to me. Always has. If that means swallowing a complaint now and then, seems like a small price."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Funny thing about weeds. Ignore one because it seems harmless, then a week later you're wondering how it reached the other side of the field. Been thinking about that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Claudia asked why I hadn't told her something was bothering me. Truth was, I didn't want to burden her. She wasn't especially impressed with that answer."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "I tell the kids they can always talk to me, even when something's difficult. Starting to notice I don't always follow my own advice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Had that conversation with Claudia. The one I'd been putting off. Wasn't pleasant. Wasn't terrible either. Mostly, I wish I'd done it sooner."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Turns out someone can be upset with you and still love you. Obvious thing to say, I know. Doesn't always feel obvious when you're trying hard not to disappoint anybody."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "I thought keeping quiet spared everyone a problem. Claudia pointed out it mostly meant she had to guess what I was thinking. Fair point."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Kira was upset with a decision I made. Usually I'd have softened it until neither of us was really saying anything. This time we talked it through. She's got a good head on her shoulders."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Milo asked me a question yesterday I didn't know how to answer. Told him that instead of making something reassuring up. He seemed perfectly satisfied with the truth."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Maybe harmony isn't everyone agreeing. Maybe it's knowing you can disagree and still come back to the same table afterward."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "I've always worried about letting my family down. Suppose that made me hide the very things they would've wanted to help me carry."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Claudia knows when something's wrong with me anyway. Thirty minutes of pretending otherwise doesn't protect her. Mostly just irritates her."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "There's trust in telling someone the part you know they might not like. Hadn't thought about honesty that way before."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Found some rot in a fence rail this morning. Looked fine from the outside. Reminded me that keeping things looking peaceful isn't much use if you're ignoring what's underneath."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "I've gotten better at saying when something bothers me. Still takes me a while. Claudia says she'll take progress over perfection."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Had a difficult talk with someone I care about recently. We both walked away understanding each other better. That's a kind of peace too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Farm's healthiest when every part gets what it needs. Families aren't much different. Sometimes what someone needs isn't agreement. It's honesty."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "I used to think a good husband and father kept worries away from his family. These days, I think he trusts them enough to face those worries together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Things aren't always peaceful at our house. Sometimes, Kira gets frustrated, Milo gets upset, Claudia and I disagree, Jasmine talks over everybody...and we're all right. Better than all right...Amazing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "You're a good friend. Means I don't have to tell you everything's fine when it isn't. Took me a while to understand how valuable that is."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "I used to think keeping people together meant smoothing over every rough patch. Now I figure strong relationships are more like good fields. You tend what's actually there, not what you wish was growing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "If something's troubling you, you can tell me. I'll listen. Might disagree, might tell you something you don't want to hear, but I won't pretend everything's fine just to keep things comfortable. Friends deserve better than that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "An interesting day. I discovered three specimens of the same fungus growing within several feet of one another, yet only one displayed the coloration described in my notes. Environmental variation, perhaps. I shall need another season of observations before drawing a conclusion."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "Ah. A word of advice while you are becoming acquainted with Blackmere: observe before assuming. The difference between a useful discovery and a foolish mistake is frequently no more complicated than looking twice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "You needn't concern yourself if I stop speaking without warning. It generally means I've noticed something. A bird, an unusual growth pattern, a change in the soil...the natural world is exceedingly inconsiderate about waiting for conversations to finish."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "You appear reasonably observant. That is intended as a compliment. Curiosity is common enough; disciplined attention is considerably rarer."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "Tell me, have you noticed whether the vegetation near the river has changed since you arrived? No need to answer immediately. Look the next time you pass through. Then tell me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I remembered something you mentioned during our previous conversation and checked one of my journals afterward. Your observation was correct. I thought you might appreciate knowing that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Before I tell you what I observed, what do you think happened? Go on. An incorrect hypothesis is perfectly respectable. Refusing to form one because it might be incorrect is considerably less useful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "You ask better questions than you did when we first met. More specific. Less concerned with finding the answer you expect. That is progress."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I encountered an unusual specimen in Gloamwood this morning. Ordinarily I would simply explain my conclusion to you. Instead, I think I'll show it to you sometime and see what you notice first."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Your perspective has become unexpectedly useful. You notice details I occasionally disregard precisely because I have observed the same subjects for so many years. Familiarity can create its own form of blindness."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "I have a question for you. Not a test, but a distinction I apparently need to make clear. I genuinely wish to know what you think."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I revised an entry yesterday because of something you pointed out. Only three sentences, mind you, but after several decades of maintaining those journals, three sentences constitute a rather significant concession."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "There are notebooks at Rain & Hale dating back to my first years in Blackmere. Some observations within them are embarrassingly primitive. I keep them anyway. One should preserve the mistakes as carefully as the discoveries."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "I've spent decades recording medicinal plants, seasonal variations, fungi, minerals, animal behavior...a considerable portion of Blackmere, really. The collection has become somewhat larger than I originally intended."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "My journals are not especially organized for another person's benefit. I know precisely where everything is. This is an excellent system provided, of course, that I remain available indefinitely."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "I call the collection The Blackmere Compendium. A grandiose title, perhaps, but after several decades and more volumes than I care to count, calling them 'Julian's notes' seemed insufficient."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "The Compendium contains observations I have never published. Not because they are secret, exactly. Many simply require context, and context is remarkably difficult to preserve on paper."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I have occasionally wondered what becomes of my journals when I am no longer here to interpret them. An unpleasant subject, perhaps, but avoiding a question has never made its answer less important."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "I attempted to reorganize part of the Compendium so another researcher could understand it. After forty minutes, I realized I was merely adding more notes explaining the previous notes. The irony was not lost on me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "One can document that a plant prefers damp soil. It is considerably harder to document the particular sheen its leaves develop when conditions are ideal. Some knowledge is easier to demonstrate than describe."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I have always assumed thorough records would be sufficient to preserve my work. Recently, that assumption has begun troubling me. A journal can contain an answer. It cannot teach someone how I learned to find it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "I showed someone one of my field techniques recently rather than simply performing it myself. They were slower, certainly. Less precise too. Then they noticed something I hadn't. An instructive experience for both of us."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "Knowledge in inexperienced hands has always made me uneasy. Yet experience cannot develop without first placing knowledge in inexperienced hands. An inconvenient contradiction."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "You've become someone I can discuss an unfinished hypothesis with. That may sound insignificant. It is not. I generally prefer my ideas thoroughly examined before allowing them into public."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---
