## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Come by Rain & Hale when you have several hours to spare. I would like to show you part of the Compendium. Several hours is not an exaggeration."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "I caught myself writing an explanation in the margin yesterday and wondering whether it would make sense to you. That is new. For most of my life, I have written primarily for myself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "There are things I know because someone once stood beside me and showed me what to look for. Strange that I have spent so much effort preserving their lessons while neglecting the method by which I received them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "I taught someone to identify a medicinal fungus this morning. Properly taught them, not merely provided its name. Watching them recognize the second specimen without assistance was...surprisingly satisfying."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "A discovery belongs to its discoverer only briefly. After that, its value depends upon whether anyone else understands it. I wish I had appreciated that distinction sooner."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I have begun leaving questions in the Compendium rather than attempting to answer every one myself. Someone should have worthwhile mysteries waiting for them after I am finished."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "You know enough now that I occasionally forget how recently you began learning some of this. That is gratifying. Also mildly alarming. I may be becoming an effective teacher."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "I used to fear that allowing others to continue my work would inevitably dilute it. Now I find myself rather hoping they'll contradict me. Provided, naturally, that they bring sufficient evidence."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Perhaps the purpose of accumulating knowledge is not to leave behind a perfect record of what I knew. Perhaps it is to leave behind people capable of discovering what I never could."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Julian - A

Dialogue: "When we first met, I generally told you what I knew. Now I find myself considerably more interested in what you will discover. I believe that qualifies as progress for both of us."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Julian - B

Dialogue: "If you ever find something the Compendium says is impossible, do not assume you are mistaken. Observe it. Record it. Test it. Then, if the evidence remains sound, correct my work. I can think of no better way to honor it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I spent much of my life believing wisdom was something one safeguarded until the proper person came along. I understand the error now. The proper person is made through teaching, patience, trust, and the freedom to ask questions I never thought to ask. I intend to spend whatever years remain ensuring there are many of them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "The flowers are opening earlier than I expected. I almost missed them this morning. It would have been a shame...small beautiful things deserve to be noticed too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "It's good to see you. I hope Blackmere has been kind to you today. And if it hasn't...well, tomorrow gets its chance."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "People sometimes think Memorial Hill must be terribly sad. It can be. But there's a great deal of love here too. I try not to let one overshadow the other."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "You're becoming a familiar face. That's a nice thing, isn't it? Seeing someone often enough that their presence begins to feel expected."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "I saw a bird earlier with the strangest little patch of white feathers. I thought you might have liked it. I'm not sure why you came to mind, but you did."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "You always seem willing to stop for a moment when we speak. Thank you for that. People underestimate how meaningful their time can be."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "I found myself wondering how your day was going. I suppose that's when an acquaintance starts becoming something more."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Anya mentioned you the other day. Nothing important, really. Just something that made her smile. Those are often the things I remember longest."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "There are people who make quiet feel uncomfortable. You don't. I appreciate that about you."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Something made me laugh yesterday, and later I wished you'd been there to hear it. That surprised me a little."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "I've noticed our conversations have become one of the pleasant parts of my day. I hope you don't mind me saying so."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "If you ever need somewhere peaceful, you're welcome to find me here. You don't have to be grieving to need a quiet place."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Ian used to tease me for stopping to admire every flower we passed. He'd walk another ten steps, realize I wasn't beside him anymore, and come back smiling."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "There are details about Ian I'm afraid of losing. His laugh. The way he'd tilt his head when he was confused. Strange how the smallest memories can feel the most precious."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "I still write letters to Ian sometimes. Things I would have told him at the end of the day. I've never told many people that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Sometimes I'll have a wonderful day with Anya, then feel guilty afterward. As though happiness somehow takes something away from the life Ian should have had with us."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "People tell me Ian would want me to be happy. They're right, of course. Knowing something and feeling it are not always the same."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "I survived the flood. Ian didn't. There are days when that still feels like something I should apologize for. I know how unfair that sounds...especially to him."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "I laughed so hard with Jasmine yesterday that my sides hurt. For one brief moment, I almost felt guilty. Then I imagined what Ian would say about that. He would have thought I was being ridiculous."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Anya deserves memories of a mother who laughed with her, doesn't she? Not only one who taught her how to remember someone she lost."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "I've spent so much time protecting old memories that I hadn't noticed how cautious I'd become about making new ones."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "I went somewhere simply because I wanted to yesterday. No obligation, no errand, no reason at all beyond thinking I might enjoy myself. I did."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "I caught myself looking forward to something next week. Then I realized I hadn't immediately felt guilty about it. That seems small, but it didn't feel small."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "Maybe moving forward isn't a road that takes us farther from the people we've lost. Perhaps we simply carry them somewhere new."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Every year before sunrise on the flood's anniversary, I bring Ian forget-me-nots. I sit beside him until the first bell and tell him everything that happened since the last time."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "My visits with Ian used to be mostly about what I missed. Lately I've found myself telling him about things that made Anya laugh, people I've grown closer to...even things I'm excited about."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "I told Ian about you recently. I think he would have liked you. He had a great deal of respect for people who cared about Blackmere."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "I've started pressing flowers from happy days, not only important ones. I like the idea of opening a book years from now and remembering an afternoon simply because it was good."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "There was a time when enjoying something new felt almost disloyal. Now, I think Ian would be far more upset if I spent the rest of my life refusing every bit of happiness offered to me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "I don't want Anya and me to build our lives entirely around the day we lost Ian. He gave us so many better days than that one."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "I used to think healing might eventually mean Ian becoming less important to me. It doesn't. The grief becomes lighter. The love doesn't."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "There are memories behind me that I will treasure for the rest of my life. I'm finally allowing myself to believe there are memories ahead worth treasuring too."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "I think Ian would recognize my life now. Different, certainly, but still full of people I love. I hope he'd be proud of the way Anya and I kept going."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Lena - A

Dialogue: "You've become part of the memories I want to keep. Not because something extraordinary happened. Because you've been here, again and again, and ordinary days with good people are worth remembering."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Lena - B

Dialogue: "I don't think happiness asks us to leave anyone behind. I can love Ian, miss him, remember him...and still love the life Anya and I have now. There's room in a heart for all of it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Lena - C

Dialogue: "Next time you have a free afternoon, come find me. Not because you need anything, and not because I have something serious to tell you. Let's simply make a good memory. I think I've spent enough years waiting for those to happen by accident."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Good to see you. I hope you're settling in well."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "You've been keeping busy. Blackmere notices dependable people."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "Another full route today. I prefer it that way."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "I see you often enough now that I've started expecting our paths to cross."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "You remember your commitments. I respect that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "Letters aren't the only things people entrust to one another. You've proven reliable with both."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "I nearly stopped by yesterday simply to say hello. Didn't have a delivery, so I reconsidered."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "You make conversation easier than most. I mean that favorably."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "I've learned quite a bit about you from speaking with you. Better than hearing it secondhand."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "I know most of Blackmere by their addresses. A few people become more than a stop along the route."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "I was hoping I'd see you today. There. Saved myself the trouble of pretending otherwise."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "A few words can carry more weight than people realize. I've appreciated ours."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "My satchel's heavier than usual today. Nothing I can't manage."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "Father says I work too hard. Coming from him, I consider that a remarkable accusation."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "People depend on me. If the work is difficult, that doesn't make it less mine to do."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Missed lunch yesterday. Too many deliveries. Helen noticed before I did."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "I don't mind carrying extra work. Asking someone else to carry it feels...less comfortable."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "You've asked whether I'm tired more than once. The honest answer is occasionally."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Mother never missed a delivery if she could help it. Even when she was ill. I think I learned more from that than she intended."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "When I was young, I decided dependable people didn't get to have difficult days. I've never quite corrected that idea."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "I don't like giving people reasons to worry about me. Somehow that became the same as never telling them when something is wrong."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Father offered to help me repair my mailbag. I nearly refused. Then I didn't."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "I asked someone to cover part of my route yesterday. Difficult sentence. Useful result."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "Accepting help feels remarkably similar to failing until you actually try it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "I kept every letter my mother wrote Father while she was ill. I still read them sometimes."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "Mother's letters weren't about pretending she was fine. She wrote when she was frightened too. I'd forgotten that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "Mother let Father worry about her. More importantly, she let him help. Perhaps I've been remembering the wrong lesson."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "I had too much to finish yesterday. I said so. Someone helped. Everything still got done."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "There's relief in saying, 'I can't manage this alone.' I wasn't expecting that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "A burden shared isn't a promise broken. I'm beginning to understand the distinction."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Father asked how I was doing. I gave him the real answer. We talked for quite a while."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "Reliability isn't doing everything yourself. It's making certain the important things are cared for. Sometimes that requires other people."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "I've spent years carrying messages between people. Strange that I needed so long to learn how to send one myself: 'I need help.'"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Martin - A

Dialogue: "You're one of the people I trust when I can't manage something alone. I don't say that lightly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Martin - B

Dialogue: "I've always believed every promise deserves to be kept. Now I know accepting someone's help can be part of keeping it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Martin - C

Dialogue: "If I ever tell you I'm fine and you know I'm not, you're permitted to ask again. That's a privilege reserved for very good friends."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "You're finding your way around Blackmere, I see. Good. A town is best learned by walking it yourself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "I could tell you how we used to do things around here. Better that you discover how things are done now."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Blackmere has changed considerably in my lifetime. That's as it should be. Towns aren't meant to stand still."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I've noticed the way you approach things. You listen before deciding. Keep that habit."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "You remind me that being new to something has its advantages. You haven't had enough time to decide what's impossible yet."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "I had some advice for you just now. Then I thought better of it. You'll find your own answer."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "You've become a familiar sight. I find myself wondering what you'll make of this town once you've known it as long as the rest of us."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "There was something I thought you might be doing the hard way. Nearly said so, too. Nearly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Agatha says there's a difference between giving people room and standing so far away they can't hear you. She may have been making a point."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "May I offer an observation? You're free to disregard it. In fact, you probably should if your own experience tells you otherwise."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "I've enjoyed watching you find your place here. Though perhaps 'watching' is the problem. A man can spend too long on the sidelines."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "If you ever want an old man's perspective, you're welcome to ask. I can't promise wisdom, but I can offer experience."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "When we were building Blackmere, we made mistakes no one remembers now. That's one advantage of age. Eventually, your worst ideas become amusing stories."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Beatrice doesn't need me telling her how to lead. I've always been certain of that. Whether that means I should never offer my thoughts is another question."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "People still ask me what I think about Blackmere. I usually turn the question back on them. Lately I've wondered whether that's always helpful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I spent so many years making decisions for this town that stepping aside felt responsible. Somewhere along the way, I may have confused stepping aside with disappearing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Younger people deserve the freedom to make their own mistakes. That doesn't mean we ought to make them repeat all of ours."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "I have opinions, you know. Quite a few of them. Agatha would happily confirm that. I've simply grown accustomed to keeping most to myself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "Someone asked my advice yesterday. I gave them an actual answer instead of a thoughtful question. Rather daring of me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Good advice shouldn't tell someone where to go. It should help them see the roads clearly enough to choose for themselves. I think I can still do that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "There's a difference between guiding someone and steering them. I spent years avoiding the first because I was afraid of doing the second."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I offered Beatrice my thoughts on something recently. She listened, disagreed with half of them, and made her own decision. Exactly as she should."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Experience is a peculiar gift. Keep it to yourself and it ends with you. Share it too forcefully and no one wants it. There's an art to the middle."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "I've begun speaking up a little more. Not to provide answers. Mostly to make certain useful questions aren't forgotten."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I keep an old map of Blackmere. Hand-drawn. It began when there were considerably fewer roads and considerably more mud."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "My map isn't particularly accurate anymore. I still add to it. New families, births, places that become important...those matter more than the roads."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "I added you to my map some time ago. Not your house. You. That's what the map has always truly been recording."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I've stopped thinking of that map as a record of the Blackmere Agatha and I built. It's a record of everyone who keeps building it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "There are places on my old map that no longer exist. That used to sadden me. Now I look at what stands there instead."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "I thought my part in Blackmere's story was largely finished. Seems I was treating age like a final page when it's merely another chapter."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "Someone younger asked me to teach them something this morning. I enjoyed it more than I expected. There are worse ways to spend one's later years."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "I don't need to shape Blackmere anymore. That's the younger generation's privilege. But I can place a few useful tools in their hands."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "I've learned something rather late in life: making room for others doesn't require making yourself absent."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I've watched you become part of Blackmere. Now I find myself looking forward to seeing what you help it become. If my experience can be useful along the way, you need only ask."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Roland - B

Dialogue: "A town isn't something one generation builds and hands to the next. We overlap. We teach, learn, disagree, improve, and leave something better together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Roland - C

Dialogue: "There's still room on my map, you know. I used to wonder who would fill it after I was gone. These days, I'd rather stay awhile and watch you all draw the next part."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "First batch came out just right this morning. Always a good way to start the day."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "Nice to see you. Things are pretty quiet around here today. Can't complain about that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "I fed the birds behind the inn before work. Bread for them, baking for everyone else. That's most of my morning accounted for."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "There you are. I'm getting used to seeing you around. That's nice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I tried something different with today's bread. Nothing dramatic, just a little thyme. Small changes are usually enough for me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "Funny how quickly someone can become part of an ordinary day. I suppose you've started doing that around here."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Had breakfast with Helen and Felix before work. Nothing special happened. Still might've been the best part of my morning."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I was wondering if you'd stop by today. Guess you're becoming one of the familiar parts of my routine."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "Some people need a lot happening around them. I've always liked days where nothing goes wrong and everybody makes it home for supper."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "I've started looking forward to our talks. They're easy. I like easy."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "You ever have one of those days where nothing remarkable happens, but you get to the end and realize you were happy? Those are good days."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "I remembered something you said while I was kneading dough earlier. Made me smile. Nearly lost count of how many folds I'd done."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Someone asked me recently if I'd ever thought about opening a bakery somewhere bigger. I said no. They looked surprised."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I've been baking in the same kitchen, seeing most of the same people, keeping mostly the same routine for years. Doesn't sound terribly exciting when I put it that way, does it?"

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "People ask what I'd do if I could do anything. I never know what answer they're expecting. I'd probably still bake tomorrow morning."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Do you think it's strange not to want more? Not more friends or more good days, I mean. More...achievement, I suppose."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "Sometimes I wonder whether everyone else sees something I don't. Bigger places to go, bigger things to become. I've never really felt pulled toward any of it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "I'm happy here. I know I am. Still...every so often someone asks what comes next, and I feel like 'this again tomorrow' must be the wrong answer."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Helen asked me why I care whether anyone thinks I'm ambitious. I didn't have much of an answer. She's annoyingly good at questions like that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I could leave Blackmere. Open my own place. Try to make a name for myself. But whenever I picture that life, mostly I wonder why I've taken myself away from one I already love."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "Maybe I've been treating contentment like something I need to defend. Seems strange, doesn't it? Being happy and then worrying whether I'm happy correctly."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Roland told me there's no sense climbing a mountain just because somebody else likes the view. I've been thinking about that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I tried listing what I'd want if I changed my life. More time with family. Work I enjoy. Good people nearby. I got halfway through before realizing I was describing this place."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "There's plenty I still want to learn. New breads, better techniques, things I haven't thought to try yet. I suppose growing doesn't have to mean leaving."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "I've kept a recipe journal since I was a boy. Thought it would be about ingredients when I started. Most of the pages have become stories instead."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "Someone once told me one of my pies reminded them of their mother's kitchen. I wrote that beside the recipe. Seems more important than remembering how long it baked."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "You've made it into my recipe journal, by the way. A few times. Don't worry, I only write down the good stories."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Made the same bread this morning that I've made hundreds of times. Still enjoyed it. I think I finally stopped expecting that to be embarrassing."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I don't think I lack dreams. Mine just aren't very loud. A warm kitchen, people I love nearby, useful work, another morning to do it all again...that's enough for me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "I've stopped saying 'just a baker.' Funny how much work one little word was doing. I'm a baker. I'm rather proud of that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Felix beat me at a board game last night. Helen laughed at me, Frederick made tea, and nobody accomplished anything important. Wonderful evening. Jazz even brought me some goat milk."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I used to wonder whether I'd look back someday and wish I'd done something extraordinary. These days I think I'll look back and be grateful for how many ordinary days I got to love."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "A good life doesn't need to impress anyone. Took me longer than it should have to realize I already knew that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Theo - A

Dialogue: "You've become one of those people I hope to see when I picture a good day. That's about as important a place as I know how to give someone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Theo - B

Dialogue: "If my life stays much like this...baking every morning, family nearby, good friends walking through the door...I'll consider myself extraordinarily fortunate."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Theo - C

Dialogue: "Tomorrow I'll wake up early, feed the birds, start the ovens, make bread, probably get flour everywhere, and see some people I love. Then I'll do something similar the day after. I can't think of a future I'd rather have."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "You're here again. Good. I prefer people who can be counted on."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "Store opens at the same hour every morning. Has for years. Folks ought to know some things won't change on them."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "You seem to be settling into Blackmere well enough. Keep showing up. That's most of what belonging requires."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "I've started recognizing your footsteps. Don't read too much into that. I recognize most regulars."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "You do what you say you'll do. Shouldn't be remarkable. Often is."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "Suppose I've gotten used to seeing you around. That's meant favorably."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "That shelf behind the counter has been there longer than some people in this town. Still holds weight. No reason to replace what's doing its job."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "People are always improving things. New this, better that. Sometimes the old way worked perfectly well."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "You don't seem bothered by my being quiet. Makes you easier company than most."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "Wasn't sure I'd see you today. Noticed when you didn't come by yesterday, too. Make of that what you will."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "You know, familiarity gets undervalued. Knowing who'll walk through the door. Knowing they'll still be themselves when they do."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "If you need something, ask. If I can help, I will. Don't expect a ceremony about it."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "Had to repair another shelf yesterday. Would've been easier to replace it. Didn't."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "Opal chose most of the fixtures in the store. Every time someone suggests changing one, I find a reason not to."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "Store doesn't look much different from when Opal was here. That's intentional."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "I know replacing an old table doesn't erase thirty years of sitting at it. Knowing isn't always the difficult part."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "Sometimes I wonder how much of this place I keep unchanged because the town needs it...and how much because I do."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "Opal liked change more than I did. She'd probably have rearranged half the store by now just to irritate me."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "My greatest fear isn't being alone. It's forgetting. Her voice. Her laugh. Some ordinary thing she said over breakfast that I didn't know I'd need to remember."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "Every familiar thing feels tied to a memory. Move enough of them and I start wondering what happens to the memories."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "I've spent a long time making sure nothing disappears. Suppose grief doesn't much care whether the thing you're protecting is a shelf or a person."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "Replaced that shelf. The new one's sturdier. Store still feels like ours."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "Moved a few things behind the counter yesterday. Spent the whole morning expecting it to feel wrong. It didn't."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "Turns out I can change something without losing what came before it. Obvious, perhaps. Still took some proving."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "I keep an opal in my pocket. Have for years. When I need a moment, I take it out and remember her."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "Funny thing. I changed part of the store, and I still remembered exactly how Opal laughed. Seems the shelves weren't holding that memory after all."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "Opal isn't in the things I refuse to move. She's in the reasons those things mattered to me in the first place."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "I've set two cups on my kitchen table every morning since Opal died. Never told anyone that."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "Second cup never gets touched. I put it away every evening and take it back out the next morning. Habit became a promise somewhere along the way."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "I'm starting to think Opal wouldn't want me keeping her place empty forever. She'd tell me cups are meant to be used."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "Set out one cup this morning. Sat there a while. Remembered Opal just fine."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "I used to think changing my routines meant admitting that life with Opal was over. It is over. Doesn't mean what we built together is."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "Opal and I wanted a store people could depend on long after us. Can't honor that dream by refusing to let it have a future."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Walter - A

Dialogue: "You've become someone I count on. Didn't intend for that to happen. Glad it did."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-walter-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Walter - B

Dialogue: "I'll always miss Opal. Don't need everything around me frozen in place to prove it anymore."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-walter-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Walter - C

Dialogue: "There's room at my table these days. If you're ever around early enough, I'll put out a second cup."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-walter-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "You have been remarkably persistent since arriving in Blackmere. Persistence is useful. Provided, of course, one knows when persistence becomes recklessness."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "I imagine you have discovered by now that rebuilding something is considerably more difficult than inheriting it. That applies to the manor as much as anything else."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 01 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "You needn't interpret my attention as suspicion. I observe carefully by nature. Though in your particular case...perhaps a degree of suspicion would be accurate."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-01-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "You seem more deliberate than I initially expected. I have not yet decided whether that reassures me, but it is worth acknowledging."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "I've noticed you rarely abandon something simply because it becomes difficult. Myrtle respected that quality. So do I."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 02 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "A person reveals quite a lot through small decisions. What they rush. What they neglect. What they take responsibility for afterward. Yours have been...interesting."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-02-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "I misjudged one thing about you. I expected considerably less patience. Do not look so pleased. One corrected assumption does not constitute an endorsement."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "You listen when people disagree with you. More importantly, you occasionally change your mind afterward. That is rarer than it should be."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 03 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "I have been reconsidering my first impression of you. Not reversing it. Reconsidering. There is an important distinction."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-03-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "You have earned a measure of my respect. I thought it better to tell you directly than leave you attempting to infer it from my behavior."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "I find our disagreements considerably less irritating than I once did. Your arguments have improved."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 04 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "There are matters I would trust you to handle without supervision now. Before you celebrate, understand that I consider that quite a significant compliment."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-04-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "A clockmaker learns quickly that force solves remarkably few problems. Apply too much pressure to the wrong component and the entire mechanism suffers. People are not entirely different."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "Myrtle used to inspect my work without saying a word. I found it infuriating. Years later, I understand she was waiting to see whether I would find the problem myself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 05 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "You remind me of Myrtle occasionally. Not in temperament. Certainly not in methodology. There is something familiar in the way you refuse to leave a problem alone."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-05-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "I know you believe the grandfather clock can be used responsibly. My objection has never been that you intend harm. Good intentions are precisely what make dangerous power so easy to justify."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "Suppose you could correct every unfortunate decision immediately. How long before accepting consequences began to seem unnecessary? That question troubles me more than the mechanism itself."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 06 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "I don't expect you to share my convictions about time. I would, however, like you to understand them. There is a difference between fearing something and respecting what it is capable of."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-06-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "You have had opportunities to use your advantages selfishly. As far as I can determine, you haven't. I confess that complicates my position."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "I expected power to make you less patient. Instead, you seem increasingly aware of when not to rely upon it. I had not adequately considered that possibility."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 07 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "Certainty is comfortable. Evidence is less considerate. It has an irritating habit of requiring us to reconsider perfectly well-organized beliefs."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-07-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "I once believed the safest answer was simply ensuring no one could interfere with time at all. That remains the simplest answer. I am no longer convinced simplicity makes it correct."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "Myrtle trusted people more readily than I do. For years, I considered that one of the few areas in which she lacked sufficient caution. I'm beginning to wonder whether I misunderstood her."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 08 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "Trust is an imprecise instrument. No measurements. No calibration. No guarantee of the result. You can imagine why I find the concept somewhat objectionable."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-08-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "This was the first pocket watch I ever built alone. It loses several minutes every hour. By any reasonable standard, it is dreadful."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "I could repair this old watch in an afternoon. I've deliberately chosen not to. It reminds me that becoming capable required being permitted to fail first."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 09 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "Myrtle never corrected this watch for me. She examined it, told me precisely how inaccurate it was, and handed it back. I thought she was being cruel. She was trusting me to become better."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-09-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "I've spent years believing responsibility meant preventing mistakes before they occurred. Perhaps sometimes it means trusting someone to recognize the mistake and choose differently."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "I still dislike the grandfather clock's power. Strongly. Yet I've realized my judgment of the mechanism and my judgment of you need not be identical."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 10 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "You once represented an uncertainty I wanted removed. Now...I would rather understand what you intend to do than decide for you what you mustn't."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-10-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "I disagreed with a decision you made recently. I considered intervening. Then I remembered that trusting someone only when they choose exactly as you would is not trust at all."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "There will probably always be choices you make that concern me. I suspect the reverse is equally true. Apparently friendship survives such inconveniences."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 11 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "Control offers certainty at the cost of another person's agency. Trust offers no certainty whatsoever. I understand now why the latter requires considerably more courage."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-11-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "When you first arrived, I watched because I believed someone needed to make certain you did no harm. I still pay attention. The difference is that now I expect you to make the responsible choice."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "I will probably never approve of manipulating time. I hope you understand that. But I trust you. Those two truths can exist together."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Dialogue - Generic - Friendship - 12 - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "Myrtle trusted you with something extraordinary. For a long time, I thought honoring her meant questioning that decision. I see it differently now. If she believed you deserved the chance to prove what sort of person you would become, then so do I. More importantly, you have."

Event: Generic

ID: dialogue_generic-friendship-12-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---
